### [1] [MotionMERGE: A Multi-granular Framework for Human Motion Editing, Reasoning, Generation, and Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18956)
*Bizhu Wu,Jinheng Xie,Wenting Chen,Zhe Kong,Jianfeng Ren,Linlin Shen,Ruibin Bai,Rong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionMERGE是一个统一框架，通过显式建模身体部位和时序级别的运动、提出推理感知的粒度协同预训练策略，以及构建细粒度运动编辑数据集MotionFineEdit，弥合了运动-语言模型在细粒度理解与局部控制方面的粒度鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动-语言模型虽然能统一理解和生成任务，但在细粒度理解和身体部位的精确控制方面存在不足，根本原因在于模型无法聚焦运动的局部模式，且训练数据缺乏细粒度监督。

Method: (1) 在单一LLM内显式建模部位级别和时序级别的运动，实现细粒度语言引导的运动控制；(2) 提出Reasoning-Aware Granularity-Synergy预训练策略，联合监督跨粒度对齐、时序定位、局部对齐、运动连贯性和运动接地思维链推理；(3) 构建MotionFineEdit数据集，包含83.7万原子三元组和14.4万复杂三元组，提供细粒度时空修正指令和运动接地CoT标注。

Result: 大量实验表明MotionMERGE在运动生成、理解和编辑任务上实现了更精确的控制，并且在其他复杂运动任务上展现出令人信服的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作代表了向更细粒度交互和类人推理的运动模型迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: Recent motion-language models unify tasks like comprehension and generation but operate at a coarse granularity, lacking fine-grained understanding and nuanced control over body parts needed for animation or interaction. This stems from fundamental issues in both the model and the data, in which the model can't focus on motion's localized pattern, and the training data lacks fine-grained supervision. To tackle this, we propose MotionMERGE, a unified framework that bridges the granularity gap. First, we pioneer the study of fine-grained languageguided motion control, including detailed understanding and localized editing, by explicitly modeling motion at part and temporal levels within a single LLM, thereby endowing the model with robust priors for precise control. Second, we design ReasoningAware Granularity-Synergy pre-training, a novel strategy that employs joint supervision for cross-granularity alignment, temporal grounding, localized alignment, motion coherency, and motion-grounded chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. This equips the model with fine-grained motion-language alignment, crossgranularity synergy, and explicit reasoning ability. Third, we curate MotionFineEdit, a large-scale dataset (837K atomic + 144K complex triplets) with the first fine-grained spatio-temporal corrective instructions and motion-grounded CoT annotations, establishing a new benchmark for fine-grained text-driven motion editing and motion-grounded reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate the capability of MotionMERGE for more precise motion generation, understanding, and editing, and compelling zero-shot generalization to other complex motion tasks. This work represents a significant step toward models that interact with motion in finer granularity and human-like reasoning.

</details>


### [2] [Artifact-Bench: Evaluating MLLMs on Detecting and Assessing the Artifacts of AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18984)
*Yuqi Tang,Yang Shi,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Xuehai Bai,Yue Ding,Ruizhe Chen,Bohan Zeng,Xinlong Chen,Xuanyu Zhu,Bozhou Li,Yuran Wang,Yifan Dai,Chengzhuo Tong,Xinyu Liu,Yiyan Ji,Yujie Wei,Yuhao Dong,Shilin Yan,Fengxiang Wang,Yi-Fan Zhang,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Artifact-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在AI生成视频的伪影检测与分析能力，包含三级层次化伪影分类体系和三个互补任务。实验发现19个主流MLLM在伪影感知和推理上存在严重局限，且与人类感知偏好显著不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型输出的视频仍存在时间不一致、结构失真、语义不连贯等伪影，而MLLM对这些伪影的感知和推理能力尚不明确。现有基准缺乏对伪影感知和细粒度诊断推理的系统评估，尤其是缺乏超越照片级真实感的多样化AI生成视频领域的评估。

Method: 1) 建立三级层次化伪影分类体系，覆盖照片级真实感、动画和CG风格视频；2) 定义三个互补任务：真实vs.AI生成视频分类、成对真实性比较、细粒度伪影识别；3) 在19个主流MLLM上进行实验评估。

Result: 19个主流MLLM在伪影感知和推理上表现出显著局限性，许多模型在挑战性设置下接近甚至低于随机水平；MLLM的判断与人类感知偏好之间存在显著的不对齐。

Conclusion: MLLM作为AI生成视频真实性的通用评估器可靠性有限，当前模型在伪影感知和细粒度推理方面仍有较大差距。

Abstract: Recent video generative models have greatly improved the realism of AI-generated videos, yet their outputs still exhibit artifacts such as temporal inconsistencies, structural distortions, and semantic incoherence. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong visual understanding capabilities, their ability to perceive and reason about such artifacts remains unclear. Existing benchmarks often lack systematic evaluation of artifact-aware perception and fine-grained diagnostic reasoning, especially across diverse AI-generated video domains beyond photorealistic content. To address this gap, we introduce Artifact-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on AI-generated video artifact detection and analysis. We first establish a three-level hierarchical taxonomy of realism artifacts, covering photorealistic, animated, and CG-style videos. Based on this taxonomy, Artifact-Bench defines three complementary tasks: real vs. AI-generated video classification, pairwise realism comparison, and fine-grained artifact identification. Experiments on 19 leading MLLMs reveal substantial limitations in artifact perception and reasoning, with many models approaching random or even below-random performance in challenging settings. We further observe significant misalignment between MLLM judgments and human perceptual preferences, highlighting their limited reliability as general evaluators for AI-generated video realism.

</details>


### [3] [EgoTraj: Real-World Egocentric Human Trajectory Dataset for Multimodal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19004)
*Ahmad Yehia,Abduallah Mohamed,Tianyi Wang,Jiseop Byeon,Kun Qian,Junfeng Jiao,Christian Claudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoTraj是一个基于Meta Quest Pro采集的户外城市环境中第一人称多模态导航轨迹数据集，包含75段序列、同步RGB视频、6自由度头部位姿、3D眼动向量和场景标注，用于评估和提升第一人称轨迹预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视角的人类轨迹预测在人形机器人、可穿戴感知和辅助导航等应用中至关重要，但由于真实环境中第一人称轨迹数据集的稀缺，该方向进展受限。为此，作者构建了EgoTraj数据集以填补这一空白。

Method: 使用Meta Quest Pro在多位置穿戴者于真实城市环境中采集数据，提供同步RGB视频、6自由度头部姿态、3D眼动向量和场景标注；并在数据集上对多种最新第一人称轨迹预测方法进行基准测试和消融研究，分析凝视、场景和运动线索的贡献。

Result: 展示了EgoTraj在增强现实感知、导航和辅助系统中的实用价值，通过基准测试和消融实验验证了数据集的有效性。

Conclusion: EgoTraj数据集及其配套工具（代码、EgoViz Dashboard）已开源，可服务于AR感知、导航和辅助系统研究。

Abstract: Accurately forecasting human trajectories from an egocentric perspective plays a central role in applications such as humanoid robotics, wearable sensing systems, and assistive navigation. However, progress in this direction remains limited due to the scarcity of egocentric trajectory datasets collected in real-world environments. Addressing this need, we introduce EgoTraj, an egocentric multimodal open dataset recorded using Meta Quest Pro (MQPro). EgoTraj contains 75 sequences of human navigation collected from multiple MQPro wearers in real-world urban environments. Each recording provides synchronized RGB video along with ground-truth data, including continuous time-synchronized 6-degree-of-freedom head poses, per-frame 3D eye gaze vectors, scene annotations. To the best of our knowledge, EgoTraj differs from typical egocentric trajectory datasets by capturing long-horizon, self-directed navigation across diverse urban routes with broad participant diversity. To demonstrate the potential of the dataset, we benchmark several state-of-the-art methods for egocentric trajectory prediction and conduct ablation studies to analyze the contributions of gaze, scene, and motion cues. The results highlight the utility of EgoTraj for AR-based perception, navigation, and assistive systems. The EgoTraj dataset, code, and EgoViz Dashboard are publicly available at https://github.com/yehiahmad/EgoTraj.

</details>


### [4] [A Systematic Failure Analysis of Vision Foundation Models for Open Set Iris Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19020)
*Rahul Anand,Siddharth Singh,Dileep A D,Mahadeva Prasanna,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种视觉基础模型在开集虹膜呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的性能，发现它们在跨数据集（相似传感器）时可迁移，但对未见攻击仪器和跨光谱场景泛化失败，LoRA微调在攻击级和光谱偏移下反而加剧失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型在多种视觉任务中表现优异，但其在虹膜呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的开集泛化能力尚未被充分研究，尤其是在真实部署中遇到的未见攻击仪器、跨传感器和跨光谱分布偏移场景下。

Method: 选取五种代表性基础模型，设计三种开集协议（未见PAI、未见数据集、NIR到VIS跨光谱），分别评估冻结特征表示和LoRA参数高效适应方法，并使用统一实验框架进行系统失败分析。

Result: 基础模型在相似传感器跨数据集场景下可迁移，但对未见攻击仪器泛化失败，跨光谱评估性能急剧下降；LoRA在部分跨数据集设置下提升性能，但在攻击级和光谱偏移下常放大失败；额外验证实验（虹膜分割输入、全参数微调、双向光谱等）确认这些失败非输入形式或单向评估的伪影。

Conclusion: 强闭集或跨数据集性能不应被视为鲁棒开集安全性的证据；需要设计能保持对呈现伪影敏感且在实际部署变化下稳定的PAD特征表示。

Abstract: Vision foundation models have demonstrated strong transferability across diverse visual recognition tasks and are increasingly considered for biometric applications. Their suitability for iris Presentation Attack Detection (PAD), particularly under realistic open-set operating conditions, remains insufficiently examined. This work presents a systematic failure analysis of general-purpose vision foundation models for open-set iris PAD using periocular imagery. Five representative foundation models are evaluated under three open-set protocols that explicitly separate different sources of distribution shift: unseen Presentation Attack Instruments (PAIs), unseen datasets captured with different sensors and cross-spectral transfer from near-infrared (NIR) to visible spectrum (VIS) imagery. Both frozen feature representations and parameter-efficient task adaptation using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) are assessed within a unified experimental framework. The results indicate that foundation models can transfer across datasets with similar sensing characteristics, but fail to generalise reliably to unseen attack instruments and degrade sharply under cross-spectral evaluation. While LoRA improves performance in certain cross-dataset settings, it frequently amplifies failure under attack-level and spectral shifts. Additional validation experiments using segmented iris inputs, full backbone fine-tuning, joint cross-dataset and cross-PAI shifts, and reverse VIS to NIR transfer further confirm that these failures are not simply artefacts of periocular input, weak adaptation, or one-directional spectral evaluation. These findings show that strong closed-set or cross-dataset performance should not be treated as evidence of robust open-set security, and highlight the need for PAD representations that maintain sensitivity to presentation artefacts while remaining stable under realistic deployment variation.

</details>


### [5] [MedFM-Robust: Benchmarking Robustness of Medical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19027)
*Xiangxiang Cui,Tianjin Huang,Yifang Wang,Lijie Hu,Lu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了医疗基础模型（MedFMs）的两大范式——医学视觉语言模型（Med-VLMs，如LLaVA-Med、GPT-4o等）和分割基础模型（基于SAM的SAM-Med2D、MedSAM等），并强调其在临床部署前需进行严格的真实世界可靠性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗基础模型在临床应用中展现出巨大潜力，但其在真实世界条件下的可靠性尚未得到充分评估，亟需系统性评测以确保临床部署的安全性。

Method: 将医疗基础模型分为两大类：医学视觉语言模型（包括医学专用模型如LLaVA-Med、MedGemma和通用模型如GPT-4o、Gemini）及基于SAM的医学分割模型（如SAM-Med2D、MedSAM），并综述其在不同医学图像理解任务（VQA、报告生成、视觉定位等）中的应用。

Result: Med-VLMs能够完成视觉问答、报告生成和视觉定位等医学图像理解任务；SAM衍生模型推动了医学分割领域的新一代发展。

Conclusion: 医疗基础模型在临床广泛部署前，必须在真实世界条件下对其可靠性进行严格评估。

Abstract: Medical foundation models (MedFMs) have emerged as transformative tools in healthcare, demonstrating capabilities across diverse clinical applications. These models can be broadly categorized into two paradigms: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) and segmentation foundation models. Med-VLMs range from medical-specialized models such as LLaVA-Med and MedGemma, to general-purpose models like GPT-4o and Gemini, all capable of medical image understanding tasks including visual question answering (VQA), report generation, and visual grounding. Concurrently, the Segment Anything Model (SAM) has catalyzed a new generation of medical segmentation models, with adaptations like SAM-Med2D and MedSAM. The widespread clinical deployment of these models thus necessitates rigorous evaluation of their reliability under real-world conditions.

</details>


### [6] [MMGS: 10$\times$ Compressed 3DGS through Optimal Transport Aggregation based on Multi-view Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19304)
*Beizhen Zhao,Sicheng Yu,Ziran Yin,Dongxu Shen,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于全局几何分布匹配的3D高斯泼溅压缩框架，通过多视图贡献排名、最优传输聚合与稠密化操作，在仅使用10%原始基元的情况下实现SOTA渲染质量，并加速训练10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS因大量冗余基元导致显著开销，现有压缩方法依赖局部采样或固定剪枝阈值，难以在减少冗余与保持高保真渲染之间取得平衡。

Method: (1) 多视图3D高斯贡献排名机制：基于几何一致性过滤基元；(2) 全局最优传输(OT)聚合算法：合并冗余基元同时保留底层几何；(3) OT-based稠密化算子：维持高斯分布属性以稳定优化。

Result: 仅使用10%基元、训练速度提升10倍，达到SOTA渲染质量。

Conclusion: 将高斯优化建模为全局几何分布匹配问题，通过OT技术有效平衡冗余减少与高质量渲染。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized 3D reconstruction, it suffers from significant overhead due to massive redundant primitives. Existing compression methods typically rely on local sampling or fixed pruning thresholds, which often struggle to balance redundancy reduction with high-fidelity rendering. To address this, we propose a novel framework that formulates Gaussian optimization as a global geometric distribution matching problem. Specifically, our approach integrates three components: (1) we introduce a multi-view 3D Gaussian contribution ranking mechanism that filters primitives using geometric consistency instead of local heuristics; (2) we propose a global Optimal Transport (OT)-based aggregation algorithm that merges redundant primitives while preserving the underlying geometry; and (3) we design an OT-based densification operator that maintains the Gaussian's distributional properties for stable optimization. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality with only \textbf{10$\%$} primitives and \textbf{10$\times$} accelerated training speeds compared to vanilla 3DGS.

</details>


### [7] [LiFT: Lifted Inter-slice Feature Trajectories for 3D Image Generation from 2D Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19060)
*Xinhe Zhang,Yuyang Zhang,Pengfei Jin,Arnau Marin-Llobet,Na Li,Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiFT是一种将3D医学图像生成分解为逐切片图像生成和切片间轨迹学习的方法，通过特征空间中的有序轨迹建模和三元平面漂移损失，在保持逐切片质量的同时实现高效高分辨率3D合成，在BraTS 2023和SynthRAD2023数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率3D医学图像生成面临两难：全3D模型计算开销大，而高效的2D切片生成器难以保持跨第三维（深度方向）的解剖一致性。现有方法在计算效率与三维一致性之间无法取得良好平衡。

Method: LiFT将3D体积合成分解为两个子任务：逐切片图像生成和切片间轨迹学习。它将体积视为特征空间中的有序轨迹，捕捉解剖结构在深度方向上的出现、变换和消失过程。采用三元平面漂移损失（tri-planar drifting loss）使生成切片的轨迹与真实体积的轨迹对齐，实现无条件生成中的分布学习；在配对翻译任务中，使用双向z-context mixer结合配准目标提供跨平面一致性，同时保持逐切片保真度。

Result: 在BraTS 2023（无条件和缺失模态MR）和SynthRAD2023（MR-to-CT）上评估。LiFT保持了逐切片质量，以约1/135的推理成本接近cWDM的缺失MR重建质量（未进行正式等效性检验），在MR-to-CT任务中相比无映射器消融基线改善了跨平面一致性。

Conclusion: 轻量级的切片间轨迹学习是实现高分辨率3D医学图像合成的可行途径，在计算效率和三维一致性之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D medical image generation remains challenging because fully volumetric models are computationally expensive, while efficient 2D slice generators often fail to preserve anatomical consistency across the third dimension. We propose LiFT, a framework for Lifted inter-slice Feature Trajectories that factorizes 3D volume synthesis into per-slice image generation and inter-slice trajectory learning. Rather than modeling the volumetric distribution end-to-end, LiFT treats a volume as an ordered trajectory in feature space, capturing how anatomical structures appear, transform, and disappear across depth. A tri-planar drifting loss aligns the trajectory of generated slices with the trajectories of real volumes, enabling distributional learning over inter-slice progressions in unconditional generation; in paired translation, a bidirectional $z$-context mixer trained against the registered target supplies through-plane coherence while preserving per-slice fidelity. We evaluate LiFT on BraTS 2023 (unconditional and missing-modality MR) and SynthRAD2023 (MR-to-CT). Across these settings, LiFT preserves per-slice quality, approaches the reported cWDM missing-MR reconstruction quality at $\sim$$135\times$ lower inference cost (without formal equivalence testing), and improves through-plane coherence on MR-to-CT relative to a no-mapper ablation, demonstrating that lightweight inter-slice trajectory learning is a viable route to high-resolution 3D medical synthesis.

</details>


### [8] [CutVerse: A Compositional GUI Agents Benchmark for Media Post-Production Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19484)
*Haobo Hu,Xiangwu Guo,Zhiheng Chen,Difei Gao,Haotian Liu,Libiao Jin,Qi Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Cutverse基准，用于评估GUI智能体在专业媒体后期制作（如Premiere Pro、Photoshop等7个应用）中的表现。包含186个复杂长程任务，现有智能体仅达到36.0%的成功率，揭示了当前模型在长程可靠性和领域特定规划方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体在网页导航和基础操作系统任务上取得了进展，但在专业创意工作流（如媒体后期制作）中的能力尚未被系统探索，存在评估空白。

Method: 构建Cutverse基准，包含7个专业应用（Premiere Pro、Photoshop等）的186个复杂长程任务，基于真实编辑工作流。开发轻量级解析器，将原始屏幕录制和低层级交互日志转换为结构化的组合式GUI动作轨迹。

Result: 现有智能体在真实媒体编辑任务上仅达到36.0%的任务成功率。当前模型在空间定位、多模态对齐和协调动作执行方面表现尚可，但在长程可靠性和领域特定规划方面仍存在显著局限。

Conclusion: Cutverse揭示了现有GUI智能体在复杂、长程的媒体后期制作工作流中的能力不足，指出了未来需要在长程可靠性和领域特定规划能力上进行突破的方向。

Abstract: While GUI agents have made significant progress in web navigation and basic operating system tasks, their capabilities in professional creative workflows remain largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce Cutverse, a benchmark designed to systematically evaluate autonomous GUI agents in realistic media post-production environments. We curate expert demonstrations across 7 professional applications (e.g., Premiere Pro, Photoshop), covering 186 complex, long-horizon tasks grounded in authentic editing workflows, involving dense multimodal interfaces and tightly coupled interaction sequences. To support scalable evaluation, we develop a lightweight parser that transforms raw screen recordings and low-level interaction logs into structured, compositional GUI action trajectories with precise grounding. Extensive evaluations reveal that existing agents achieve only 36.0\% task success on realistic media editing tasks, underscoring the challenges posed by complex, long-horizon media post-production workflows in our benchmark.While current models demonstrate promising spatial grounding, multimodal alignment, and coordinated action execution, they remain limited in long-horizon reliability and domain-specific planning.

</details>


### [9] [CRAFT: Critic-Refined Adaptive Key-Frame Targeting for Multimodal Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19075)
*Mahesh Bhosale,Abdul Wasi,Vishvesh Trivedi,Pengyu Yan,Akhil Gorugantu,David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRAFT是一种面向多视频问答的查询条件化管道，结合动态关键帧选择、多语言ASR和混合评判循环，在MAGMaR 2026上取得最佳性能（平均0.739，引用F1 0.635），并在WikiVideo上泛化良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实新闻事件的多视频问答需要系统从异构视频档案中提取查询相关证据，并为每个声明标注来源，现有方法缺乏系统化的证据聚合与引用验证机制。

Method: CRAFT管道包含：(1) 动态关键帧选择；(2) 每视频ASR+多语言回退；(3) 混合评判循环（UNLI时序蕴涵、DeBERTa-v3跨声明筛选、Llama-3.2-3B裁决器）进行迭代验证与修复；(4) 最终引用合并阶段，每个事实仅输出一次并附带所有支持来源标识。

Result: MAGMaR 2026上：整体平均0.739、引用召回0.810、引用F1 0.635，均为最佳；WikiVideo上平均0.823；消融实验表明原子声明、ASR和评判循环是主要增益来源。

Conclusion: CRAFT的以声明为中心的证据聚合方法在多个基准上表现优异且泛化良好，验证了迭代评判与引用合并机制在多视频问答中的有效性。

Abstract: Grounded multi-video question answering over real-world news events requires systems to surface query-relevant evidence across heterogeneous video archives while attributing every claim to its supporting source. We introduce CRAFT (Critic-Refined Adaptive Key-Frame Targeting), a query-conditioned pipeline that combines dynamic keyframe selection, per-video ASR with multilingual fallback, and a hybrid critic loop to iteratively verify and repair claims before consolidation. The pipeline integrates UNLI temporal entailment, DeBERTa-v3 cross-claim screening, and a Llama-3.2-3B adjudicator, with a final citation-merging stage that emits each fact once with all supporting source identifiers. On MAGMaR 2026, CRAFT achieves the best overall average (0.739), reference recall (0.810), and citation F1 (0.635). We further evaluate on a MAGMaR-style conversion of WikiVideo with 52 non-overlapping event queries, where CRAFT also performs strongly (0.823 Avg), showing that its claim-centric evidence aggregation generalizes beyond MAGMaR. Ablations show that atomic claims, ASR, and the critic loop drive the main gains over the vanilla query-conditioned baseline. Code and implementation details are publicly available at https://github.com/bhosalems/CRAFT.

</details>


### [10] [Knowing When Not to Predict: Self Supervised Learning and Abstention for Safer DR Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19133)
*Muskaan Chopra,Lorenz Sparrenberg,Jan H. Terheyden,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了自监督预训练时长对医学图像模型在置信度校准和选择性预测（基于置信度的弃权）中的影响，发现预训练长度并不总是提升可靠性，因此应将其作为与可靠性相关的设计选择来对待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL研究主要关注下游准确率，但对于糖尿病视网膜病变分级等安全关键任务，模型还需知道何时预测不可靠并弃权给临床审查，SSL预训练时长对置信度校准和弃权行为的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 在固定微调协议下，评估多个不同预训练时长的SSL检查点，通过校准置信度、覆盖率、选择性准确率和选择性宏F1等指标，分析预训练时长对置信度校准和基于置信度弃权的影响。

Result: SSL预训练相比从头训练能改善选择性预测；但准确率饱和后，选择性性能在不同检查点间仍会显著变化，且更长的预训练并不总能持续提升可靠性。

Conclusion: 预训练时长应被视为与可靠性相关的关键设计选择，而不仅是计算细节；研究强调了弃权感知评估的重要性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is now a standard way to pretrain medical image models, but performance is still mostly judged by downstream accuracy. For safety-critical screening tasks such as diabetic retinopathy grading, this is not enough: a model must also know when its predictions are unreliable and defer uncertain cases for clinical review. In this work, we examine how the length of SSL pretraining influences calibrated confidence and confidence-based abstention. We evaluate multiple SSL checkpoints under a fixed fine-tuning protocol and assess calibrated confidence, coverage, selective accuracy, and selective macro-F1. Across datasets and data regimes, SSL pretraining improves selective prediction compared to training from scratch. Unlike prior SSL studies that primarily evaluate downstream accuracy or AUROC, we analyze how SSL pretraining duration influences confidence behavior under calibrated confidence-based abstention. However, once accuracy saturates, selective performance can still change markedly across checkpoints, and longer pretraining does not consistently improve reliability. These results underscore the importance of abstention-aware evaluation and suggest that pretraining length should be treated as an important reliability-related design choice rather than only a computational detail. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [11] [Towards Data-Efficient Video Pre-training with Frozen Image Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19137)
*Svetlana Orlova,Niccolò Cavagnero,Gijs Dubbelman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了一种轻量级视频模型训练范式：冻结预训练的图像基础模型，仅训练循环时序模块来处理流式视频，从而在不进行大规模视频预训练的情况下实现强时序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型需要大规模视频数据和计算资源进行预训练，而图像基础模型已具备强大的空间表征能力。作者希望探究能否通过复用图像基础模型的空间表征，仅针对时序推理进行轻量级训练来构建有竞争力的视频模型，从而大幅降低数据和计算成本。

Method: 冻结预训练的图像基础模型作为空间编码器，在其之上训练一个循环时序模块（recurrent temporal module）来处理流式视频输入。仅时序模块参与训练，空间编码器保持冻结状态。

Result: 在多个视频理解任务上的实验表明，即使没有大规模视频预训练，该方法也能产生强大的时序性能，验证了该轻量级训练范式的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来研究提供了方向：通过在冻结的图像基础模型上预训练时序模块来构建循环视频基础模型，这是一种高效且有前景的视频建模路径。

Abstract: Video foundation models achieve strong performance across many video understanding tasks, but typically require large-scale pre-training on massive video datasets, resulting in substantial data and compute costs. In contrast, modern image foundation models already provide powerful spatial representations. This raises an important question: can competitive video models be built by reusing these spatial representations and pre-training only for temporal reasoning? We take initial steps toward exploring a lightweight training paradigm that freezes a pre-trained image foundation model and trains only a recurrent temporal module to process streaming video. By reusing an image foundation model as a spatial encoder, this approach could significantly reduce the amount of video data and compute required compared to end-to-end video pre-training. In this work, we explore the feasibility of this approach before investing in computing for video pre-training. Our empirical findings across multiple video understanding tasks suggest that strong temporal performance can emerge without large-scale video pre-training, motivating future work on recurrent video foundation models obtained by pre-training a temporal module on top of a frozen image foundation model. Code: https://github.com/tue-mps/towards-video-image-frozen .

</details>


### [12] [Smartphone-based Circular Plot Sampling for Forest Inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19213)
*Su Sun,Jui-Cheng Chiu,Nabin Khanal,Songlin Fei,Yingjie Victor Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于智能手机的单次步行视频完成圆形样地树木测量方法，集成单目深度估计、树木实例分割与SLAM框架，实现了无需专用硬件的树木位置和胸径精准测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统森林样地调查中，树木胸径和空间位置的精确测量要么依赖昂贵的地面激光雷达系统，要么依赖耗时的卡尺和罗盘人工方法，两者在大规模环境中的可扩展性和可及性均受限。

Method: 将预训练的单目深度估计和树木实例分割与SLAM框架结合，联合优化视频序列中的相机轨迹和深度；通过融合SLAM相机位姿与分割深度图恢复树木位置和胸径，并用校准参考长度确定绝对尺度。

Result: 在人工林样地中平均绝对误差1.51 cm（MARE 3.98%），在天然林样地中为2.30 cm（MARE 5.69%），在不同起始方向和位置下表现一致，跨视频一致性分析也显示了稳定的可重复性。

Conclusion: 该方法在达到与已建立野外方法相当的精度的同时，大幅降低了设备成本和操作复杂度，使专业研究人员和非专业森林管理者都能在多样化操作场景中使用。

Abstract: Circular sample plots are a cornerstone of forest inventory, yet accurate measurement of tree diameter at breast height (DBH) and spatial location within such plots remains challenging. Conventional approaches rely either on costly terrestrial LiDAR systems or labor-intensive manual methods involving calipers and compass bearings, limiting their scalability and accessibility in large scale environments. We present a lightweight, smartphone-based pipeline that enables complete plot sampling based tree measurement from a single walkthrough video, requiring no specialized hardware beyond a consumer smartphone mounted on a portable stand. The proposed method integrates pretrained monocular depth estimation and tree instance segmentation with a simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) framework to jointly refine camera trajectories and depth across the video sequence. Tree positions and DBH estimates are recovered by fusing SLAM-derived camera poses with segmented depth maps, with absolute real-world scale anchored via a calibrated reference length.
  The system was evaluated in both managed forest plots and natural forest plot, achieving a mean absolute error of 1.51 cm (MARE 3.98%) and 2.30 cm (MARE 5.69%) respectively, with consistent performance across varying starting directions and positions. Cross-video consistency analysis further demonstrated stable and reproducible tree localization across measurements initiated from different starting positions. The proposed approach achieves accuracy comparable to established field methods while substantially reducing equipment cost and operational complexity, making it accessible to both professional researchers and non-expert forest managers in diverse operational settings.

</details>


### [13] [HAVEN: Hierarchically Aligned Multimodal Benchmark for Unified Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19223)
*Mengqi Shi,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAVEN是一个层次化对齐的多模态视频理解基准，包含全粒度（帧、镜头、视频）和全模态（视频、文本）的标注架构，并配套了涵盖摘要、时序推理、多模态定位和显著性排序的综合评估套件。对现有MLLM的基准测试揭示了表面文本流畅性与深层多模态理解之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在复杂叙事总结与推理方面的评估不足，已有摘要基准将监督信号分散在孤立粒度（关键帧、关键镜头或碎片化文本摘要）上，未能捕捉跨模态对齐内在的层次化结构。

Method: 构建HAVEN数据集：采用全粒度（帧级、镜头级、视频级）和全模态（视频和文本）的标注架构，实现模态间的显式连续对齐。在此基础上设计覆盖摘要、时序推理、多模态定位和显著性排序的综合评估套件。

Result: 对多种SOTA MLLM的广泛基准测试表明，模型在表面文本流畅性与扎根的多模态理解之间存在持续性的性能差距。

Conclusion: HAVEN将多模态系统评估推进到传统QA格式之外，为可解释的层次化视频理解研究提供了严格、标准化的测试平台。数据集、基准套件和评估协议均已公开。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit strong performance on standard video tasks, their ability to faithfully summarize and reason over complex narratives remains poorly evaluated. Existing summarization benchmarks fragment supervision across isolated granularities, such as keyframes, key shots, or disjointed text summaries, failing to capture the inherently hierarchical structure of cross-modal alignment. To address this critical gap, we introduce HAVEN, a hierarchically aligned multimodal benchmark for unified video understanding. HAVEN pioneers a fully granular (frame, shot, and video levels) and fully multimodal (video and text) dataset architecture, complete with explicit, continuous alignment between modalities. Built upon this unified annotation paradigm, we propose a comprehensive evaluation suite spanning summarization, temporal reasoning, multimodal grounding, and saliency ranking. Extensive benchmarking of state-of-the-art MLLMs exposes a persistent gap between surface-level textual fluency and grounded multimodal understanding. Ultimately, HAVEN advances the evaluation of multimodal systems beyond traditional QA formats, offering a rigorous, standardized testbed to drive future research in interpretable, hierarchical video understanding. We publicly release the dataset, benchmark suite, and evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [14] [Robust Mitigation of Age-Dependent Confounding Effects via Sample-Difficulty Decorrelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19230)
*Nikhil Cherian Kurian,Victor Caquilpan Parra,Abin Shoby,Luke Whitbread,Lyle J. Palmer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种鲁棒框架，通过解耦年龄与疾病相关的样本难度趋势，而非强制年龄不变性，来减轻医学图像分类中年龄导致的诊断偏差（过度诊断/欠诊断），并降低对年龄分布偏移的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类中存在年龄依赖的性能差异：年龄作为混杂因素，关联成像形态与疾病患病率，导致高患病率年龄段的过度诊断和低患病率年龄段的欠诊断，且年龄分布偏移会加剧这一问题。现有强制年龄不变性的方法可能抑制临床上有意义的年龄信息。

Method: 1）预热阶段后，以标签为条件刻画样本难度并建模其年龄依赖趋势；2）使用Huber加权亲和权重解耦年龄与主导的年龄-难度趋势，削弱混杂驱动的捷径，同时保留有临床意义的非线性年龄信息；3）引入年龄覆盖分数（Age Coverage Score），根据小批量年龄方差缩放解耦惩罚项，确保低年龄多样性下的稳定优化。

Result: 在两个放射学数据集上，该方法能有效降低年龄相关的真阳性率和假阳性率差异，且对AUC影响极小；在训练-测试年龄分布偏移加剧时仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过有选择地削弱年龄作为混杂因素带来的虚假趋势而非完全消除年龄信息，可以在减少年龄相关诊断偏差的同时保持模型性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Age dependent performance disparities in medical image classification often arise because age acts as a confounder, linking imaging morphology with disease prevalence. In practice, disparities can manifest as overdiagnosis at ages where disease prevalence is higher and underdiagnosis at ages where prevalence is lower, and can worsen under train test shifts in the age distribution. Conventional mitigation approaches that enforce strict age invariance may suppress diagnostically meaningful information encoded in age. We therefore propose a robust framework that mitigates the effects of age-dependent confounding by targeting spurious age linked trends rather than enforcing invariance. Following a warm-up phase, we characterize sample difficulty and model its age-dependent trends in a label-conditioned manner. We decorrelate age from dominant age difficulty trends using robust, Huber weighted affinity weights, attenuating confounding-driven shortcuts while preserving clinically meaningful, nonlinear age information. We further introduce an Age Coverage Score that scales the decorrelation penalty by minibatch age variance to ensure stable optimization under limited age diversity. Across two radiology datasets, our approach reduces age dependent true and false positive disparities with minimal AUC impact and remains robust to increasing train test age distribution shifts.

</details>


### [15] [PhyWorld: Physics-Faithful World Model for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19242)
*Pu Zhao,Juyi Lin,Timothy Rupprecht,Arash Akbari,Chence Yang,Rahul Chowdhury,Elaheh Motamedi,Arman Akbari,Yumei He,Chen Wang,Geng Yuan,Weiwei Chen,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyWorld，一种通过两阶段后训练（流匹配微调+基于直接偏好优化的物理对齐）提升视频生成模型物理保真度的世界模拟器，在VBench和物理保真度基准上均取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模拟器需要物理可信的视频延续，但现有视频生成模型难以保证生成内容符合基本物理规律，限制了其作为物理AI训练环境的可靠性。

Method: 两阶段后训练：第一阶段使用流匹配微调提升视频到视频的延续能力，稳定视觉属性和运动动力学；第二阶段利用直接偏好优化（DPO）在物理偏好对上对齐生成动力学，提升物理合理性。

Result: 在VBench上平均得分0.769（基线≤0.756），在物理保真度基准上平均得分3.09（最强基线2.99）。

Conclusion: 对大规模视频生成模型进行延续性和物理偏好信号的后训练，可使其成为更有效的物理AI世界模拟器。

Abstract: World simulators can provide safe and scalable environments for training Physical AI systems before real-world deployment. Large video generation models are emerging as a promising basis for such simulators because they can generate diverse and realistic visual futures. However, using them as world simulators requires physically faithful video continuations, namely, generated videos that preserve the physical state implied by the conditioning input, and evolve in ways consistent with basic physical principles. We propose PhyWorld, a video generation world model designed to produce temporally coherent and physically faithful scene continuations through two-stage post-training. In the first stage, we improve video-to-video continuation with flow matching fine-tuning, encouraging stable visual attributes and coherent motion dynamics across frames. In the second stage, we align generated dynamics with physical principles using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) over physics preference pairs, guiding the model toward outputs with higher physical plausibility. To evaluate PhyWorld, we use both standard video-quality benchmarks and a dedicated physical-faithfulness benchmark with per-law scoring. Experiments show that PhyWorld improves video consistency, achieving an average score of 0.769 on VBench compared with 0.756 or below for state-of-the-art baselines. PhyWorld also improves physical plausibility, reaching an average score of 3.09 on our physical-faithfulness benchmark compared with 2.99 for the strongest baseline. These results suggest that post-training large video generation models with continuation and physics-preference signals can make them more effective world simulators for Physical AI.

</details>


### [16] [Structuring Open-Ended NAS: Semi-Automated Design Knowledge Structuring with LLMs for Efficient Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19247)
*Yuiko Sakuma,Masakazu Yoshimura,Marcel Gröpl,Zitang Sun,Junji Otsuka,Atsushi Irie,Takeshi Ohashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FairNAD方法，通过半自动化结构化模型设计知识引导神经架构搜索，利用LLM从论文中提取知识构建多样化搜索空间，并结合多类型变异策略高效探索，在三个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS方法受限于预定义且限制性的搜索空间；LLM辅助的NAS方法虽能实现开放式搜索空间，但因设计想法存在偏差或质量低下导致探索效率不佳。

Method: 首先定义高层架构属性的结构化模板，然后利用LLM分析论文来填充该模板，构建丰富多样的搜索空间；提出FairNAD，采用多类型变异策略实现高效探索，包括公平思想采样变异、Pareto感知变异、LLM驱动的迭代变异以及细粒度反馈循环。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet16-120上分别比当前最优方法提升0.84、2.17和2.35个百分点。

Conclusion: FairNAD通过半自动化结构化设计知识并结合多类型变异策略，能够有效发现高性能架构。

Abstract: Current neural architecture search (NAS) methods are often limited by their predefined, restrictive search spaces. While recent large language model (LLM)-assisted NAS methods enable open-ended search spaces, they often suffer from inefficient exploration due to biased or low-quality design ideas. To address these issues, we propose to semi-automatically structure model design knowledge to guide the search process. Our approach first defines a high-level structural template of architectural attributes. An LLM then populates this template by analyzing papers, creating a rich and diverse search space that embodies this structured design knowledge. To efficiently explore this vast space, we introduce FairNAD, using a multi-type mutation that enables broad exploration through mutation with fair idea sampling, Pareto-aware mutation, LLM-driven iterative mutation, and a fine-grained feedback loop. We demonstrate the effectiveness of FairNAD in discovering high-performing architectures that yield 0.84, 2.17, and 2.35 points improvement on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet16-120, respectively, compared to current state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [17] [What Makes Synthetic Data Effective in Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19289)
*Jinjin Zhang,Xiefan Guo,Yizhou Jin,Nan Zhou,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩散模型生成的合成图像在视觉分割任务中的效用，发现具有密集场景组成和精细实例保真度的合成图像能产生更具判别性的空间表征。基于此提出SENSE统一框架，通过灵活可扩展的合成数据显著提升分割性能，该框架与多种架构兼容且能有效跨模型规模扩展，在Cityscapes、COCO和ADE20K上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能生成逼真图像，但它们在复杂视觉分割任务中的潜力尚未被充分探索，因此需要系统分析合成图像对分割任务的有效性及其关键影响因素。

Method: 首先对最先进的扩散模型生成的合成图像进行系统分析，识别出密集场景组成和精细实例保真度是关键因素；然后提出SENSE统一框架，利用灵活可扩展的合成数据增强分割性能，该框架与DPT、Mask2Former等多种架构兼容。

Result: SENSE框架在Cityscapes、COCO和ADE20K三个数据集上验证了有效性，展示了良好的泛化能力，且能与不同参数容量的模型有效扩展。

Conclusion: 合成数据（尤其是具有密集场景和精细实例保真度的图像）可显著提升视觉分割任务性能，SENSE框架提供了一种模型无关、可扩展的有效方案。

Abstract: Driven by rapid advances in large-scale generative models, synthetic data has emerged as a promising solution for visual understanding. While modern diffusion models achieve remarkable photorealistic image synthesis, their potential in complex visual segmentation tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we conduct a systematic analysis of synthetic images from state-of-the-art diffusion models to uncover the factors governing their utility. In particular, synthetic images characterized by dense scene composition and fine instance fidelity demonstrate distinctive benefits, yielding significantly more discriminative spatial representations. Building on these insights, we propose SENSE, a unified framework that leverages flexible and scalable synthetic data to substantially enhance segmentation performance. Notably, SENSE is model-agnostic, compatible with diverse architectures (e.g., DPT and Mask2Former), and scales effectively across models with varying parameter capacities. Extensive experiments on Cityscapes, COCO, and ADE20K validate the effectiveness and generalization capability of our approach. Code is available at https://github.com/zhang0jhon/SENSE.

</details>


### [18] [iGSP:Implicit Gradient Subspace Projection for Efficient Continual Learning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19301)
*Xuezhi Cui,Dongbo Zhou,Wang Guo,Zeyuan Wang,Ziyu Li,Gaozhi Zhou,Xian Li,Ling Zhao,Wentao Yang,Chao Tao,Haifeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出iGSP框架，通过隐式梯度子空间投影实现视觉-语言模型的高效持续学习，在MTIL基准上达到SOTA准确率，同时训练参数减少42.7%，总参数减少86.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法存在两个问题：1）为每个任务分配独立模块导致参数爆炸；2）基于视觉相似性的共享机制错误地将表层视觉相似等同于底层对齐一致性，导致视觉相似但逻辑不同任务间的负迁移，以及未能利用视觉不同任务间的对齐复用。

Method: iGSP包含两个阶段：1）子空间识别阶段：通过MoE路由器的早期收敛建立子空间基，利用基预扩展引入候选专家，应用子空间约束正则化将新任务梯度隐式投影到历史子空间，通过将路由概率视为梯度流指标来精确剪枝冗余维度；2）正交子空间微调阶段：固定结构基并移除正则化，快速拟合任务特定残差损失。

Result: 在MTIL基准上达到SOTA准确率；训练参数减少42.7%；最终总参数减少86.9%。

Conclusion: 将对齐共享问题重新定义为共享低秩子空间内优化轨迹重叠的几何问题，通过隐式梯度子空间投影实现了高效的参数共享和知识复用。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models require efficient adaptation to continually emerging downstream tasks. While Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning mitigates catastrophic forgetting, assigning isolated modules per task leads to parameter explosion. Conversely, recent similarity-driven sharing mechanisms falsely equate superficial visual similarity with underlying alignment consistency. This fundamental mismatch triggers severe negative transfer between visually similar but logically distinct tasks and fails to exploit alignment reuse across visually diverse ones. We argue thatalignment sharing is fundamentally a geometric problem of overlapping optimization trajectories within shared low-rank subspaces. Grounded in this insight, we propose iGSP, a novel framework that achieves efficient adaptation via implicit gradient subspace projection. Leveraging the early convergence of MoE routers to establish the subspace basis, iGSP bifurcates the adaptation process into two phases. First, the Subspace Identification phase introduces candidate experts via basis pre-expansion, applies a novel subspace-constrained regularization to implicitly project new task gradients onto the historical subspace, and precisely prunes redundant dimensions by treating routing probabilities as gradient flow indicators, ultimately to maximize knowledge reuse. Second, the Orthogonal Subspace Fine-Tuning phase fixes this structural basis and removes the regularization to rapidly fit the task-specific residual loss. Extensive experiments on the MTIL benchmark demonstrate that iGSP achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while significantly improving training efficiency, reducing the average trainable parameters by 42.7\% compared to current SOTA methods, and decreasing the final total parameters by 86.9\% relative to counterparts. The source code is available at https://github.com/GeoX-Lab/iGSP.

</details>


### [19] [MetaRA: Metamorphic Robustness Assessment for Multimodal Large Language Model-based Visual Question Answering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19307)
*Quanxing Xu,Yuhao Tian,Ling Zhou,Xian Zhong,Xiaohua Huang,Rubing Huang,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MetaRA 框架，利用蜕变测试系统性地评估多模态大模型在 VQA 任务中的鲁棒性，揭示传统准确率指标无法发现的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VQA 评估依赖静态数据集和准确率指标，无法衡量模型的鲁棒性、一致性和泛化能力。

Method: 基于蜕变测试（MT），定义蜕变关系（MRs）生成受控的图像-问题输入变体，对 MLLM 驱动的 VQA 系统进行系统性探测。

Result: MetaRA 暴露了模型对语言扰动的敏感性、对浅层视觉线索的过度依赖以及多模态推理的深层缺陷，提供了比传统准确率指标更丰富的诊断信息。

Conclusion: 需要系统性的鲁棒性评估，蜕变评估作为一种可扩展、模型无关的方法，有助于构建可信的多模态 AI。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA), as the representative multimodal task, serves as a key benchmark for evaluating the reasoning capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, existing evaluations largely rely on static datasets and accuracy-based metrics, which fail to capture robustness, consistency, and generalization. Inspired by Metamorphic Testing (MT), we propose Metamorphic Robustness Assessment (MetaRA), a testing framework that employs Metamorphic Relations (MRs) to systematically probe vulnerabilities in MLLM-based VQA systems. MetaRA generates controlled variations of image-question inputs based on specific MRs and evaluates models across diverse conditions. Applying MetaRA to multiple MLLM-based VQA models across different tasks reveals nuanced failure patterns, including sensitivity to linguistic perturbations, over-reliance on superficial visual cues, and deeper weaknesses in multimodal reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that MetaRA provides richer diagnostic insights than conventional accuracy metrics, exposing failure modes that remain hidden under standard benchmarks. Overall, this work highlights the need for systematic robustness evaluation in VQA and positions metamorphic assessment as a scalable, model-agnostic approach toward trustworthy multimodal AI.

</details>


### [20] [SWEET: Sparse World Modeling with Image Editing for Embodied Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19319)
*Yiren Song,Yihan Wang,Xiyao Deng,Zhuoran Yan,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SWEET框架，利用图像编辑模型（而非视频生成模型）作为稀疏视觉世界模型，通过逐步生成任务相关的操作关键帧，并将其转换为机器人可执行的动作，实现了更高效、更可靠的具身控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集视频生成计算成本高，且对于许多操作任务而言，大多数帧是冗余的，任务的进度可由少量任务相关的视觉状态概括。因此，研究图像编辑模型能否作为稀疏视觉世界模型来预测任务级未来状态。

Method: 1) 对比Wan2.2（视频生成）与FLUX-Kontext（图像编辑），发现图像编辑在关键帧预测上更优；2) 提出SWEET框架：通过连续图像编辑逐步生成任务相关操作关键帧，结合语言指令和可选箭头空间引导；3) 使用目标条件扩散动作预测器将相邻关键帧转化为可执行动作块；4) 引入混合训练策略，使用过滤后的编辑目标来减少真实与编辑视觉子目标之间的差异。

Result: 在DROID和RoboMimic数据集上，SWEET在见过和未见场景中均改进了关键帧预测，并实现了从序列关键帧规划到可执行机器人动作的完整流程。

Conclusion: 图像编辑是具身视觉预测中一个有前景且尚未充分探索的方向。

Abstract: Visual prediction has emerged as a promising paradigm for embodied control, where future observations are generated and then translated into actions. However, dense video generation is computationally expensive and often unnecessary for many manipulation tasks, whose progress can be summarized by a small number of task-relevant visual states. In this work, we study whether image editing models can serve as sparse visual world models for robot manipulation by predicting task-level future states without dense video rollout. We first conduct a controlled comparison between the video generation model Wan2.2 and the image editing model FLUX-Kontext under the same robotic data setting, and find that image editing produces more reliable task-level keyframes with better visual fidelity and substantially lower inference cost. Motivated by this observation, we propose SWEET, a one-shot sparse visual planning framework that progressively generates a sequence of task-relevant manipulation keyframes through successive image editing, conditioned on language instructions and optional arrow-based spatial guidance. A goal-conditioned diffusion action predictor then converts adjacent imagined keyframes into executable action chunks. To reduce the mismatch between real and edited visual subgoals, we further introduce a mixed-training strategy with filtered edited targets. Experiments on DROID and RoboMimic show that SWEET improves keyframe prediction across seen and unseen scenes and enables a full pipeline from sequential keyframe planning to executable robot actions, suggesting that image editing is a promising and underexplored direction for embodied visual prediction.

</details>


### [21] [TextAlign: Preference Alignment for Text Rendering with Hierarchical Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19320)
*Mingxuan Cui,Jingpu Yang,Fengxian Ji,Qian Jiang,Zhecheng Shi,Jiaming Wang,Zirui Song,Fajri Koto,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextAlign，一种非侵入式的后训练偏好对齐框架，通过分层VLM奖励信号（全局、单词、字形三级）将文本渲染错误转化为偏好信号，结合GRPO/DPO优化，在保持生成器架构不变的前提下提升文本渲染准确性，实验在FLUX.1-dev和Z-Image-Turbo上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型文生图模型在忠实文本渲染方面持续存在弱点，现有方法多依赖架构特定模块或编码器修改，增加了跨基础模型的部署复杂性。本文旨在探索一种无需修改生成器架构的通用解决方案。

Method: 将文本渲染视为后训练偏好对齐问题，提出TextAlign框架。核心组件为基于分层VLM的奖励模型，将渲染错误分解为全局、单词和字形三个层级，将二元缺陷判断转化为标量偏好信号，该信号支持GRPO和DPO两种优化策略。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Z-Image-Turbo上实验，基于OCR的文本准确性指标持续提升，且不损害通用生成质量。与SD3.5、Qwen-Image、AnyText、TextDiffuser等强基线相比，验证了奖励设计作为模型重设计的可扩展替代方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 奖励设计为改进文本渲染提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，非侵入式框架避免了架构修改，便于跨基础模型部署，同时保持或提升了文本渲染性能。

Abstract: Faithful text rendering remains a persistent weakness of large text-to-image generative models, as it requires both semantic instruction following and fine-grained glyph-level structure. Prior methods often improve this ability through architecture-specific modules or encoder modifications, which complicate deployment across foundation models. We study text rendering as a post-training preference-alignment problem and propose TextAlign, a non-invasive framework that keeps the generator architecture unchanged. The key component is a hierarchical vision-language model (VLM)-based reward that decomposes rendering errors into global, word, and glyph levels, then converts binary defect judgments into a scalar preference signal. The resulting signal supports both Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Z-Image-Turbo show consistent gains in OCR-based text accuracy without degrading general generation quality. Compared with strong foundation and text-rendering baselines, including SD3.5, Qwen-Image, AnyText, and TextDiffuser, these results indicate that reward design offers a scalable alternative to model redesign for improving text rendering.

</details>


### [22] [RE-VLM: Event-Augmented Vision-Language Model for Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19329)
*Hanqing Liu,Mingjie Liu,Luoping Cui,Endian Lin,Donghong Jiang,Chuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RE-VLM是首个双流视觉-语言模型，联合利用RGB图像和事件流，在正常和恶劣条件下实现鲁棒场景理解。通过图驱动管道生成场景图及字幕/问答对，在构建的PEOD-Chat和RGBE-Chat数据集上，优于同参数量RGB-only和Event-only模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLM在恶劣环境（低光、高动态范围、快速运动）下性能退化，而事件相机可补充RGB的不足。现有工作缺乏有效融合两种模态并缺乏RGB-事件-文本对齐数据。

Method: 提出RE-VLM：(1) 双流架构（并行RGB编码器和事件编码器）+ 渐进式训练策略对齐异构视觉特征与语言；(2) 图驱动管道，将同步RGB-事件流转化为可验证场景图，并合成字幕和QA对；(3) 构建PEOD-Chat（光照挑战场景）和RGBE-Chat（多样化场景）两个数据集。

Result: 在字幕生成和VQA基准上，RE-VLM以可比参数量一致优于SOTA的RGB-only和Event-only模型，在恶劣条件下提升尤其显著。

Conclusion: 事件增强的VLM能有效实现跨真实环境的鲁棒视觉-语言理解，验证了双流融合策略的有效性。

Abstract: Conventional vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to interpret scenes captured under adverse conditions (e.g., low light, high dynamic range, or fast motion) because standard RGB images degrade in such environments. Event cameras provide a complementary modality: they asynchronously record per-pixel brightness changes with high temporal resolution and wide dynamic range, preserving motion cues where frames fail. We propose RE-VLM, the first dual-stream vision-language model that jointly leverages RGB images and event streams for robust scene understanding across both normal and challenging conditions. RE-VLM employs parallel RGB and event encoders together with a progressive training strategy that aligns heterogeneous visual features with language. To address the scarcity of RGB-Event-Text supervision, we further propose a graph-driven pipeline that converts synchronized RGB-Event streams into verifiable scene graphs, from which we synthesize captions and question-answer (QA) pairs. To develop and evaluate RE-VLM, we construct two datasets: PEOD-Chat, targeting illumination-challenged scenes, and RGBE-Chat, covering diverse scenarios. On captioning and VQA benchmarks, RE-VLM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art RGB-only and event-only models with comparable parameter counts, with particularly large gains under challenging conditions. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of event-augmented VLMs in achieving robust vision-language understanding across a wide range of real-world environments. Code and datasets are available at https://github.com/bupt-ai-cz/RE-VLM.

</details>


### [23] [Selective, Regularized, and Calibrated: Harnessing Vision Foundation Models for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19340)
*Junyuan Ma,Xunzhi Xiang,Wenbin Li,Qi Fan,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HERA（分层示例表示自适应）框架，通过三阶段（选择-正则化-校准）流水线，在不重新训练源数据的情况下，利用VFM实现跨域小样本分割，仅微调不到2.7%的参数，在多个基准上以超过4.1 mIoU超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VFM在跨域小样本分割（CD-FSS）中存在两大挑战：(1) 每类少量标注样本相对于VFM预训练规模过小，导致微调时过拟合；(2) 目标域偏移在预训练中未充分体现，造成跨域不一致性和层敏感性。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出HERA框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 分层层级选择（HLS），通过基于数据的示例迁移风险（ETR）自适应选择最具信息量的VFM层；(2) 先验引导正则化（PGR），对所选表示进行正则化交互，产生良好结构的局部信号；(3) 像素级自适应校准（PAC），将所选表示与精炼交互图结合进行像素级预测校准。整个流水线冻结VFM特征，仅微调<2.7%参数。

Result: 在多个CD-FSS基准上，HERA以超过4.1 mIoU的优势超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: HERA通过分层选择-正则化-校准的流水线，有效解决了VFM在跨域小样本分割中的过拟合和域偏移问题，实现了高效、无需源数据重训练的域适应分割。

Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) have achieved strong performance across various vision tasks. However, it still remains challenging to apply VFMs for cross-domain few-shot segmentation (CD-FSS), which segments objects of novel classes under domain shifts using only a few labeled exemplars. The challenge is mainly driven by two factors: (1) limited labeled exemplars per novel class relative to the scale of VFM pre-training, making the model prone to overfitting during retraining, and (2) target-domain shifts underrepresented during pre-training, inducing cross-domain inconsistency and layer-wise sensitivity. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Exemplar Representation Adaptation (HERA), a three-stage select-regularize-calibrate VFM-based segmentation framework that learns effectively from limited labels and adapts to novel domains without source-data retraining. We first design Hierarchical Layer Selection (HLS) to adaptively identify the most informative VFM layer using a data-dependent Exemplar Transfer Risk (ETR) computed for each candidate layer. Then, Prior-Guided Regularization (PGR) regularizes interactions on the selected representation, yielding well-structured local signals for the subsequent stage. Furthermore, Pixelwise Adaptive Calibration (PAC) combines the selected representation with the refined interaction maps to calibrate pixel-wise predictions, producing consistent masks. Together, these stages form a hierarchical select-regularize-calibrate pipeline that guides frozen VFM features in new domains while fine-tuning less than 2.7% of parameters at test time. Extensive experiments show that HERA surpasses the state of the art by more than 4.1 mIoU across multiple CD-FSS benchmarks.

</details>


### [24] [Semantic-Enriched Latent Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19342)
*Tianrun Xu,Yue Sun,Qixun Wang,Jingyi Lu,Yuan Wang,Tianren Zhang,Longteng Guo,Fengyun Rao,Jing Lyu,Feng Chen,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLVR是一个两阶段学习框架，通过细粒度属性监督和M-GRPO对齐策略，在紧凑的潜空间中丰富语义表示，提升多模态潜空间推理的鲁棒性和语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜空间推理方法依赖视觉监督，产生的潜表示语义丰富度不足，难以支持多样化的区域级推理任务。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段在细粒度属性监督下学习语义丰富的区域中心潜变量；第二阶段采用多查询组相对策略优化（M-GRPO）对齐同一区域上多个查询的潜表示。同时构建了包含约40万区域级属性标注和80万多查询问答样本的SLV-Set数据集。

Result: SLVR相比现有基线方法，在潜空间视觉推理的鲁棒性和语义一致性上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过语义丰富的潜表示和多查询对齐策略，SLVR有效提升了潜空间视觉推理在语义变化下的表现。

Abstract: Multimodal latent-space reasoning aims to replace explicit thinking with images by performing visual reasoning directly in a compact latent space. However, existing approaches largely rely on visual supervision and produce latent representations that lack sufficient semantic richness, limiting their ability to support diverse region-level reasoning tasks. In this work, we introduce Semantic-Enriched Latent Visual Reasoning (SLVR), a two-stage learning framework that enriches latent representations with attribute-level visual semantics and aligns them with diverse reasoning objectives. In the first stage, SLVR learns semantically enriched region-centric latents under fine-grained attribute supervision. In the second stage, we design Multi-query Group Relative Policy Optimization (M-GRPO) to align latent representations across multiple queries grounded in the same region. To support this framework, we construct SLV-Set, comprising approximately 400K region-level attribute annotations and 800K multi-query question answering samples, and introduce SV-QA, a benchmark that evaluates latent reasoning under semantic variation. Experiments demonstrate that SLVR improves the robustness and semantic consistency of latent visual reasoning compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [25] [MAM-CLIP: Vision-Language Pretraining on Mammography Atlases for BI-RADS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19359)
*Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAM-CLIP多模态模型，利用2,313张乳腺钼靶图像与对应文本描述，通过对比学习预训练视觉编码器，在BI-RADS评分预测任务上显著提升性能，尤其在标注样本稀缺时提升幅度达+14%（3类平均F1）。研究发现，来自乳腺钼靶图谱的2K图文对比2K标注样本更具信息量（平均提升+1.1%），并公开了预处理数据集与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺钼靶图像解读存在主观差异，仅依赖图像标签训练分类模型性能有限，需要引入文本描述中的丰富信息来增强视觉编码器对乳腺影像发现的理解。

Method: 从两个乳腺钼靶图谱中收集2,313张图像及其对应描述文本，使用预训练PubMedBERT作为语言编码器，通过对比学习在图文对上进行预训练，使视觉编码器吸收文本信息，再分别在两个数据集上微调用于BI-RADS评分预测。

Result: 在BI-RADS 3类平均F1上提升+1%到+14%（40K样本时+1%，1K样本时+14%）；2K图文对比2K标注样本平均提升+1.1%（训练样本超10K时）。

Conclusion: 提出MAM-CLIP视觉-语言模型，证明了乳腺钼靶图谱中的文本信息对提升模型性能具有重要价值，并在标注稀缺时效果尤为显著。

Abstract: Deep learning methods have demonstrated promising results in predicting BI-RADS scores from mammography images. However, the interpretation of these images can vary, leading to discrepancies even among radiologists. Given the inherent complexity of mammograms, training classification models solely on image labels often yields limited performance. To address this challenge, we curated 2313 mammogram images and their corresponding captions from two mammography atlases. Our proposed approach employs a multi-modal model that uses a pretrained PubMedBERT as the language component. By training this model on image-text pairs with contrastive learning, we enable the vision encoder to absorb the rich information contained in the captions, thereby improving its understanding of mammography findings. We then fine-tune the vision encoder on two datasets for BI-RADS prediction, achieving superior performance compared with models trained without this pretraining, particularly when labeled samples are scarce. The improvement in the 3-class average F1 score ranges from +1% to +14%: a +1% increase with 40K training samples, and a +14% increase with 1K samples. Furthermore, our experiments reveal that 2K image-text pairs from mammography atlases can be more informative than 2K labeled samples for label prediction, with an average margin of +1.1% when more than 10K training samples are available. Overall, our work provides a vision-language model for mammography and highlights the value of textual information from mammography atlases. In addition, we publicly release preprocessed mammography images of the TEKNOFEST dataset. The training code, pre-trained model weights, data extraction scripts, and the released dataset are publicly available at: https://github.com/igulluk/MAM-CLIP

</details>


### [26] [Scalable, Energy-Efficient Optical-Neural Architecture for Multiplexed Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19360)
*Parnian Ghapandar Kashani,Shiqi Chen,Aydogan Ozcan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合数字-模拟深度伪造视频检测框架，结合轻量级数字前端与空间复用的光学解码后端，通过可编程空间光调制器实现大规模并行模拟推理。该系统可在单次光学传播中同时处理15路以上视频流，在Celeb-DF数据集上达到97.79%的平均检测准确率，并展现出对视频退化、噪声、压缩、实验失调及黑盒对抗攻击的鲁棒性，实现了吞吐量、能效与对抗鲁棒性的同步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的深度伪造检测方法依赖计算密集且能耗高的推理算法，限制了其可扩展性。亟需一种高效、可信的检测系统以应对AI生成视觉媒体的快速 proliferation。

Method: 提出混合数字-模拟深度伪造视频检测框架：轻量级数字前端负责初步特征提取，空间复用的光学解码后端通过可编程空间光调制器在单次光学传播中并行处理15路以上视频流，实现大规模并行模拟推理。

Result: 在可见光谱空间复用实验装置上，于Celeb-DF视频数据集实现97.79%平均检测准确率、99.86%敏感度、95.72%特异度，15路视频单次光学传播并行测试。系统对视频退化、噪声、压缩、实验失调及黑盒对抗攻击均具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将光学计算集成到AI推理中，能够同时实现吞吐量、能效和对抗鲁棒性的提升——这三个特性在纯数字系统中难以兼得。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of AI-generated visual media has created an urgent need for efficient, trustworthy deepfake detection systems. However, existing deep learning-based detection methods rely on computationally intensive and energy-demanding inference algorithms, limiting their scalability. Here, we present a hybrid digital-analog deepfake video detection framework that combines a lightweight digital front-end with a spatially multiplexed optical decoding back-end for massively parallel analog inference through a programmable spatial light modulator. By simultaneously processing 15 or more video streams within a single optical propagation pass, the system enables high-throughput and accurate video-level authenticity prediction at reduced computational cost compared with purely digital methods. We validated this hybrid deepfake video processor using different datasets spanning classical face-swapping, real-world deepfake recordings, and fully AI-generated videos. Using a spatially multiplexed experimental set-up operating in the visible spectrum, we achieved average deepfake detection accuracy, sensitivity and specificity of 97.79%, 99.86% and 95.72%, respectively, on the Celeb-DF video dataset with 15 videos tested in parallel in a single optical pass per inference. The multiplexed optical decoder also demonstrates resilience against various types of video degradation, noise, compression, experimental misalignments and black-box adversarial attacks. Our results show that integrating optical computation into AI inference enables simultaneous gains in throughput, energy efficiency, and adversarial robustness - three properties that are difficult to achieve together in purely digital systems.

</details>


### [27] [Multi-Scale Generative Modeling with Heat Dissipation Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19371)
*Jun Ma,Hanquan Zhang,Yanjun Qin,Haoyuan Guan,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出热耗散流匹配（HDFM），将连续模糊（热耗散）过程引入流匹配框架，通过插值热耗散路径解决不适定问题，并采用x预测缓解高维回归困难，在图像生成任务中优于多数基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模糊扩散的模型局限于SDE框架，未集成到ODE框架（如流匹配）中；经典逆热耗散过程存在不适定挑战；在高维噪声/速度空间中回归模糊图像也存在困难。

Method: 提出HDFM：将连续热耗散（模糊）过程引入流匹配框架；设计插值热耗散路径以解决不适定性；采用x预测（而非速度预测）来缓解高维回归困难。

Result: 消融实验和玩具实验表明HDFM同时受益于模糊扩散和x预测；在多个数据集上性能优于大多数基线方法。

Conclusion: HDFM成功将基于模糊的生成模型扩展到ODE框架，通过多尺度先验注入和x预测策略有效解决了不适定和高维回归问题，显著提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models are widely used in image generation, with most relying on noise-based corruption and denoising. A distinct branch instead uses blur as the main corruption, preserving better color budgets and multi-scale detail by providing multi-scale priors. However, blur-based models remain in SDE-based frameworks and are not integrated into ODE-based frameworks, such as Flow Matching (FM). Meanwhile, in the blur-based formulation, the classical inverse heat-dissipation (IHD) process faces an ill-posed challenge. Moreover, under the data-manifold assumption, regressing blurred images from high-dimensional noise (or velocity) space is also difficult. We propose Heat Dissipation Flow Matching (HDFM), which introduces a continuous blurred (heat-dissipation) process into FM to inject multi-scale priors. HDFM aligns an interpolated heat-dissipation path to address ill-posedness and adopts $x$-prediction to mitigate high-dimensional regression difficulty. Toy experiments and ablation studies show that HDFM consistently benefits from both blur and $x$-prediction. The performance of HDFM outperforms most baseline methods on all datasets.

</details>


### [28] [Concept-Guided Noisy Negative Suppression for Zero-Shot Classification and Grounding of Chest X-Ray Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19374)
*Chenyu Lian,Hong-Yu Zhou,Chun-Ka Wong,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoNNS提出了一种概念引导的噪声负样本抑制框架，通过构建层级概念本体论（含41个关键临床概念）实现细粒度跨患者对的重新标注，并设计概念感知NCE损失函数，在胸部X光零样本分类和定位任务上超越现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对比学习将不同患者的影像和报告简单视为负对，但不同患者常表现相似影像学发现，这种噪声负样本导致语义模糊，降低了零样本理解任务的性能。

Method: (1) 利用大语言模型构建层级概念本体论（41个关键临床概念，建模存在性、属性、文本证据）；(2) 跨患者对重新标注策略：细粒度分解、噪声负样本过滤、难负样本挖掘；(3) 概念感知NCE损失（Concept-Aware NCE loss）在对齐视觉-文本特征的同时抑制噪声负样本。

Result: 在多粒度零样本定位任务和五个零样本分类数据集上，CoNNS优于现有SOTA模型。

Conclusion: 通过概念引导的噪声负样本抑制可以有效解决对比学习中的语义歧义问题，提升医学影像零样本理解的性能。

Abstract: Vision-language alignment using chest X-rays and radiology reports has emerged as an advanced paradigm for zero-shot classification and grounding of chest X-ray findings. However, standard contrastive learning typically treats radiographs and reports from different patients simply as negative pairs. This assumption introduces noisy negatives, as different patients frequently exhibit similar findings. Such noisy negatives cause semantic ambiguity and degrade performance in zero-shot understanding tasks. To address this challenge, we propose CoNNS, a concept-guided noisy-negative suppression framework. To support the negative suppression mechanism, unlike previous methods that use raw reports or templatized texts, we construct a hierarchical concept ontology using large language models. The ontology structures 41 key clinical concepts by explicitly modeling presence, attributes (location and characteristics), and texts (evidential segment and presence statement). Leveraging this ontology, we implement a cross-patient pair relabeling strategy comprising three steps: (1) Fine-Grained Breakdown to categorize pairs based on finding presence; (2) Noisy Negative Filtering to resolve semantic conflicts by removing false negatives; and (3) Hard Negative Mining to identify subtle attribute discrepancies using a lightweight language model. Finally, we propose a Concept-Aware NCE loss to align visual features with text while suppressing the identified noisy negatives. Extensive experiments across multi-granularity zero-shot grounding tasks and five zero-shot classification datasets validate that CoNNS outperforms existing state-of-the-art models. The code is available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/conns.

</details>


### [29] [MatPhys: Learning Material-Aware Physics Parameters for Deformable Object Simulation from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19386)
*Yang Yang,Yiyan Wang,Zheming Liu,Naoya Iwamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatPhys提出了一种从单视角视频预测弹簧-质量参数的材质感知前馈框架，通过DINO特征进行语义部件分解和材质先验查询，结合可学习的材质码本实现跨场景一致性，将欠约束的单目问题转化为基于共享材质概念的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理驱动的方法可以从视频中恢复物理数字孪生，但存在两个根本局限：1）通常假设整个物体为均质材料；2）场景特定的逆优化结合单目观测的固有歧义性，导致同一材质在不同场景或交互中产生不一致的参数。

Method: 1）利用DINO特征将物体分解为语义有意义的部件并查询部件级材质先验；2）引入可学习的材质码本作为外观与物理之间的桥梁，实现共享材质嵌入；3）使用部件级先验作为参考分布约束解码器，确保同材质在不同场景下产生一致参数。

Result: 在重建和未来预测任务中与逐场景优化基线方法相当，在未见交互和物体上的泛化能力更强，物理参数一致性更好。

Conclusion: 通过共享、可复用的材质概念将欠约束的单目问题转化为前馈推理，有效解决了均质材料假设和跨场景参数不一致这两个根本问题。

Abstract: Reconstructing simulation-ready deformable objects is important for vision, graphics, and robotics. Existing physics-driven methods can recover physical digital twins from videos, but they suffer from two fundamental limitations: they typically assume a homogeneous material across the whole object, and their scene-specific inverse optimization, combined with the inherent ambiguity of monocular observation, yields inconsistent parameters for the same material across different scenes or interactions. We propose MatPhys, a material-aware feed-forward framework that predicts spring-mass parameters from a single-view video, addressing these two issues with two coupled designs. To relax the homogeneous material assumption, we use DINO features to decompose the object into semantically meaningful parts and to query a part-level material prior, assigning each part its own physical behavior. To enforce cross-scene consistency, we introduce a learned material codebook of shared material embeddings as the bridge between appearance and physics, and further use the part-level prior as a reference distribution that constrains the decoder so that the same material yields consistent parameters across scenes and interactions. Together, these designs turn an under-constrained monocular problem into feed-forward inference grounded on shared, reusable material concepts. Experiments show that our method matches per-scene optimization baselines in reconstruction and future prediction, while achieving stronger generalization to unseen interactions and objects with more consistent physical parameters.

</details>


### [30] [LMM-Track4D: Eliciting 4D Dynamic Reasoning in LMMs via Trajectory-Grounded Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19390)
*Chaoyue Li,Yongxue Xu,Jie Feng,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了轨迹引导的多轮时空对话任务，构建了Track4D-Bench基准数据集（526个对话样本，23.5k帧，7.5k对象标注），并提出了LMM-Track4D模型，该模型结合射线-时间几何编码（RTGE）、专用流式状态令牌TRK和对象槽运动学残差锚定解码器（OSK-RA），在4D连续时空动态推理上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态模型（LMM）在图像和视频理解上能力增强，但仍难以进行4D连续时空动态推理，存在能力差距。

Method: 提出LMM-Track4D，包含三个核心组件：射线-时间几何编码（RTGE）用于时空特征编码；专用流式状态令牌TRK用于长时序动态传播；对象槽运动学残差锚定解码器（OSK-RA）用于在遮挡和视角变化下进行稳定的4步3D状态估计。

Result: 在Track4D-Bench上的实验表明，LMM-Track4D相较于强基线模型取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式的动态状态建模是激发LMM进行4D动态推理的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Recent large multimodal models (LMMs) have become increasingly capable on image and video understanding, yet still struggle to sustain 4D continuous spatiotemporal dynamic reasoning. To study this capability gap, we formulate trajectory-grounded multi-turn spatiotemporal dialogue, a new task in which a model must answer spatiotemporal queries while returning structured 3D target trajectories over an entire short clip or a specified segment of a longer clip, and introduce Track4D-Bench, a benchmark with 526 clip-level dialogue samples spanning 23.5k frames and 7.5k object annotations, for training and evaluation. Building on this task, we propose LMM-Track4D, which combines RTGE (Ray--Time Geometry Encoding), a dedicated streaming state token TRK for long-horizon dynamic propagation, and an Object-Slot Kinematic, Residual-Anchor (OSK-RA) decoder for stable 4-step 3D state estimation under occlusion and viewpoint variation. Experiments on Track4D-Bench show consistent improvements over strong baselines, suggesting that explicit dynamic state modeling is a useful design principle for eliciting 4D dynamic reasoning in LMMs. Our code and dataset will be publicly available at https://github.com/mikubaka88/LMM-Track4D.

</details>


### [31] [Neuron Incidence Redistribution for Fairness in Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19393)
*Abin Shoby,Lyle John Palmer,Nikhil Cherian Kurian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现迁移学习模型中，倒数第二层主导激活通道在正预测时共激活疾病阳性样本和特权人口群体（男性、年长患者），导致过度诊断；负预测时共激活弱势群体（女性、年轻患者），导致系统性漏诊。为此提出神经元发病率重分布（NIR），通过惩罚预测概率加权平均激活值的方差来强制扩散疾病证据，无需人口统计标签即可显著降低TPR/FPR差异并小幅提升AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类模型在不同人口属性（年龄、性别、种族）上存在严重的亚组性能差异，即人口统计公平性问题。现有方法多需人口统计标签或复杂对抗训练，缺乏对偏差内在神经表征机制的理解和轻量级无标签解决方案。

Method: 1) 识别偏差机制：迁移学习模型中正预测的主导倒数第二层通道同时响应疾病阳性样本和特权群体，负预测主导通道响应弱势群体；2) 提出NIR：对倒数第二层神经元计算预测概率加权平均激活值，并对其施加方差惩罚项，促使疾病证据在全部神经元上均匀分布，无需人口统计标签。

Result: 在HAM10000数据集上，年龄组TPR差异从10.81%降至0.93%，性别TPR差异从12.04%降至0.74%，AUC提升0.51点；在Harvard OCT-RNFL数据集上，种族FPR差异从15.68%降至10.66%，年龄FPR差异从12.69%降至1.80%。

Conclusion: 将潜在疾病证据分布在倒数第二层全连接神经元上是一种有原则且有效的策略，能在无需人口统计标签的情况下显著提升医学AI的人口统计公平性。

Abstract: Deep learning models for medical image classification are susceptible to subgroup performance disparities across demographic attributes such as age, gender, and race. We identify a latent representational mechanism underlying these disparities: in transfer-learned models, the dominant penultimate-layer activation channel under positive predictions is co-activated by both disease-positive samples and privileged demographic groups (male, older patients), producing over-diagnosis; conversely, the dominant channel under negative predictions is co-activated by disadvantaged groups (female, younger patients), producing systematic under-diagnosis. To address this, we propose Neuron Incidence Redistribution (NIR), a lightweight regularization method that penalizes the variance of predicted-probability-weighted mean activations across penultimate-layer neurons, requiring no demographic labels at training time. On HAM10000, TPR disparity drops from 10.81% to 0.93% across age groups and from 12.04% to 0.74% across gender, with a marginal AUC improvement of 0.51 points. On Harvard OCT-RNFL, NIR reduces FPR disparity for race (from 15.68% to 10.66%) and age (from 12.69% to 1.80%), demonstrating that distributing latent disease evidence across the full penultimate layer is a principled and effective strategy for improving demographic fairness in medical AI.

</details>


### [32] [Targeted Downstream-Agnostic Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19446)
*Zhuxin Lei,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对预训练编码器的目标性下游无关攻击方法（TDAA），通过引入"威胁图像"作为特征级锚点，生成示例特定的对抗扰动，使编码器对对抗样本和威胁图像输出相同特征，从而在更严格的威胁模型下实现目标性且下游无关的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下游无关攻击（DAA）方法威胁模型过于宽松，仅要求改变原始预测而不要求特定目标；此外，下游任务未知且编码器不直接输出预测，使得实现目标攻击极具挑战性。

Method: 提出TDAA方法，引入预选"威胁图像"作为目标；设计生成器为每个样本生成示例特定的对抗扰动，迫使编码器对对抗样本和威胁图像输出相同特征表示，从而在特征层面建立任务无关的攻击桥梁。

Result: 在10种自监督方法、3个基准数据集上的广泛实验验证了该方法的有效性，并揭示了预训练编码器存在显著脆弱性。

Conclusion: 通过引入更严格的目标性威胁模型和示例特定扰动生成策略，TDAA能够有效攻击预训练编码器，揭示了现有编码器在安全方面的严重缺陷。

Abstract: Recently, pre-trained encoders have gained widespread use due to their strong capability in representation extraction. However, they are vulnerable to downstream-agnostic attacks (DAAs). Existing DAA methods operate under a permissive threat model, where an attack is successful if the generated downstream-agnostic adversarial examples (DAEs) change the original prediction, without requiring a specific target. In this paper, we propose a Targeted DAA (TDAA) method under a stricter threat model requiring the attack to be both targeted and downstream-agnostic. Since the downstream task is unknown and encoders do not directly produce predictions, achieving a targeted attack is particularly challenging. To address this, we introduce a novel component termed the 'threat image', pre-selected by the attacker as the target. Specifically, a generator is designed to produce example-specific adversarial perturbations that compel the victim encoder to output identical features for both the DAEs and the threat image. Unlike previous DAA methods that generate a single shared perturbation for all samples, which often fails due to image diversity, our method adopts an example-specific paradigm. This generates tailored perturbations for each image to ensure a high attack success rate and invisibility. By leveraging the threat image as a feature-level anchor, our method builds a task-agnostic bridge to reveal the vulnerabilities of the victim encoder. Extensive experiments on 10 self-supervised methods across 3 benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach and reveal the pronounced vulnerability of pre-trained encoders. The code will be made publicly available after the review period.

</details>


### [33] [EventPrune: Cascaded Event-Assisted Token Pruning for Efficient First-Person Dynamic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19506)
*Pengtao Ma,Ziliang Zhou,Ciyu Ruan,Haoyang Wang,Kaiyuan Li,Zihang Gong,Wenhua Ding,Chen Gao,Jingao Xu,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Event Cascade Pruning (ECP)，首个利用事件相机连续运动先验引导视觉token剪枝的训练无关框架，在减少80% token情况下超越全token基线，并引入首个真实RGB-事件第一人称空间推理基准ESR-Real。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer-based Video-LLMs中密集视觉token带来二次方注意力计算成本，现有token剪枝范式依赖离散静态快照，无法保留推理所需的运动与几何线索。

Method: ECP包含三个阶段：Event-Triggered Causal Sampling（事件触发因果采样锚定运动信息关键帧）、Event-guided Motion Saliency Filtering（事件引导运动显著性滤波抑制事件不活跃token）、Event-Attention Ranking Fusion（事件注意力排序融合校准空间注意力与运动显著动态）。

Result: 80%视觉token减少下，ECP超越全token基线（37.62% vs 36.31%），实现1.89x推理加速和52% GFLOPs降低；在ESR-Real基准上准确率提升2.68个百分点。

Conclusion: 利用事件相机连续运动先验进行token剪枝可有效提升第一人称动态空间推理的效率与准确性，且方法无需训练即可应用。

Abstract: First-person dynamic spatial reasoning requires models to track continuous motion and precise geometric structure, but the quadratic attention cost of Transformer-based Video-LLMs makes dense visual tokens computationally expensive. Existing token pruning paradigms predominantly rely on discrete static snapshots, failing to preserve the motion and geometric cues essential for reasoning. We propose Event Cascade Pruning (ECP), to our knowledge the first training-free framework that leverages the high-frequency motion cues from event cameras as a continuous event-guided motion prior to guide token selection. ECP combines three stages: Event-Triggered Causal Sampling to anchor motion-informative keyframes, Event-guided Motion Saliency Filtering to suppress event-inactive visual tokens, and Event-Attention Ranking Fusion to calibrate spatial attention with motion-salient dynamics. With 80% visual token reduction, ECP outperforms the full-token baseline (37.62% vs. 36.31%) while achieving 1.89x inference speedup and 52% GFLOPs reduction. We further introduce ESR-Real, the first real-world RGB-event benchmark for first-person spatial reasoning, where ECP improves accuracy by 2.68 percentage points over full-token baselines.

</details>


### [34] [Return of Frustratingly Easy Unsupervised Video Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19510)
*Pengfei Wei,Yiqun Sun,Zhiqiang Xu,Yiping Ke,Lawrence B. Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MetaTrans，一种简单但高效的無監督視頻域自適應方法，通过时域静态减法模块分别处理空间和时间域差异，在两个损失项下实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督视频域自适应（UVDA）是一个实用但研究不足的问题，现有方法复杂且性能有限。

Method: 提出MetaTrans方法，包含简洁的学习目标（仅两个基本损失项），通过时域静态减法模块的模型架构设计，分别处理跨域视频的空间和时间差异。

Result: 在多个跨域动作识别任务上，MetaTrans实现了显著的绝对适应性能提升，并且相比现有UVDA基线方法取得了明显更优的相对性能增益。

Conclusion: MetaTrans验证了通过简洁的损失函数和精巧的架构设计即可有效解决UVDA问题，分离处理时空差异是有效的策略。

Abstract: Unsupervised video domain adaptation (UVDA) is a practical but under-explored problem. In this paper, we propose a frustratingly easy UVDA method, called MetaTrans. Specifically, MetaTrans adopts a concise learning objective that contains only two fundamental loss terms. Despite the simplicity of the learning objective, MetaTrans embodies an advanced UVDA idea, that is, handling the spatial and temporal divergence of cross-domain videos separately, through a subtle model architecture design. By implementing a temporal-static subtraction module, MetaTrans effectively removes spatial and temporal divergence. Extensive empirical evaluations, particularly on various cross-domain action recognition tasks, show substantial absolute adaptation performance enhancement and significantly superior relative performance gain compared with state-of-the-art UVDA baselines.

</details>


### [35] [Are Watermarked Images Editable? SafeMark for Watermark-Preserving Text-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19511)
*Xiaodong Wu,Qi Li,Xiangman Li,Zelin Zhang,Lingshuang Liu,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeMark框架，通过将阈值化的水印解码损失直接加入扩散编辑器的训练目标，在保持高质量语义编辑的同时确保水印完整性，证明了语义可编辑性与水印完整性在根本上是兼容的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一个基础但未被充分研究的问题：带水印的图像能否在编辑后仍保持水印完整性，即实现可编辑的水印图像。

Result: 在多个数据集、文本引导编辑方法和编辑后失真设置下，SafeMark实现了高水印比特准确率，同时保持高质量语义编辑，且不牺牲对常见编辑后失真的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义可编辑性与水印完整性在根本上是兼容的，SafeMark使得生成编辑流程中可信的图像溯源成为可能。

Abstract: This paper investigates a fundamental yet underexplored question: can watermarked images remain editable without compromising watermark integrity? We propose SafeMark, a framework for watermark-preserving text-guided image manipulation that explicitly integrates watermark integrity into the editing process. Specifically, SafeMark adds a thresholded watermark-decoding loss directly to the diffusion editor's training objective, fine-tuning the editor so that semantically valid edits also preserve the embedded watermark at the final output. This design admits a clean information-theoretic justification: maintaining high bit-accuracy on the edited image lower-bounds the mutual information that the editor channel preserves between watermark and edited output, the quantity that fundamentally controls watermark recoverability. SafeMark is compatible with differentiable diffusion-based editors, and requires no architectural modification. Extensive evaluations across multiple datasets, text-guided editing methods, and post-edit distortion settings demonstrate that SafeMark achieves high watermark bit accuracy across diverse editing settings while maintaining high-quality semantic edits, without sacrificing robustness to common post-edit distortions. These results demonstrate that semantic editability and watermark integrity are fundamentally compatible, enabling trustworthy image provenance in generative editing pipelines.

</details>


### [36] [iDiff: Interpretable Difference-aware Framework for Pairwise Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19522)
*Xinli Yue,JianHui Sun,Tao Shao,Liangchao Yao,Fan Xia,Yuetang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出iDiff框架，采用Answer Model和Thinking Model双分支设计，分别进行偏好预测和理由生成，在NTIRE 2026 RAIM挑战中取得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业摄影中的成对图像质量评估不仅需要判断哪张图像更优，还需要提供令人信服的、基于图像的理由。现有方法缺乏对差异的显式建模和结构化解释能力。

Method: iDiff采用双分支架构：Answer Model通过分解左右图像的全局和局部视图，结合内容感知专业化（人物/场景）和集成聚合进行鲁棒偏好预测；Thinking Model利用专家风格模板、多源质量特征和基于Answer Model预测的答案感知监督进行理由生成。

Result: 在NTIRE 2026 RAIM挑战中取得第一名，在准确率和推理质量指标上均表现出色。

Conclusion: 显式差异建模与结构化多模态推理相结合能有效提升成对IQA的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Pairwise image quality assessment (IQA) in professional photography requires a model not only to identify the preferred image between two candidates, but also to provide convincing and image-grounded reasoning. In the NTIRE 2026 RAIM challenge, this requirement is further emphasized by jointly evaluating preference prediction and rationale generation. To address this task, we propose iDiff, an Interpretable Difference-aware framework for pairwise image quality assessment. Our method adopts a dual-branch design consisting of an Answer Model and a Thinking Model. The Answer Model performs robust preference prediction by explicitly decomposing each sample into left/right global and local views, followed by content-aware specialization for person and scene images and ensemble-based aggregation across backbones. The Thinking Model focuses on rationale generation and is progressively enhanced with expert-style templates, multi-source quality features, and answer-aware supervision conditioned on the Answer Model prediction. In this way, iDiff jointly models discriminative decision making and structured explanation, improving both robustness and interpretability. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework on both accuracy and reasoning-quality metrics. Our method achieved first place in the NTIRE 2026 RAIM challenge, showing the effectiveness of integrating explicit difference modeling with structured multimodal reasoning for pairwise IQA.

</details>
