We address this question in the domain of Natural Language Processing by leveraging two data resources we create: a naturalistic corpus of over 37,000 papers from the ACL Anthology (2020-2024); and a synthetic dataset of 3,000 human-written passages and their LLM-generated improvements. We first implement a series of diachronic lexical analyses, showing that both word frequency and usage contexts have changed significantly over time, indicating semantic specialization in some cases and generalization in others. Broadening our perspective, we then model a range of more complex stylistic features and find that LLM-modified texts more frequently contain certain syntactic constructions, more complex and longer words and a lower lexical diversity. Finally, we connect these changes in writing practices to subjective reading experience through a pilot annotation study with 20 domain experts. They overall rate LLM-improved texts as more understandable and exciting, but also express negative qualitative attitudes towards LLMs, highlighting the strongly subjective effect of AI-assisted writing on reading experience.

</details>


### [146] [FlexDraft: Flexible Speculative Decoding via Attention Tuning and Bonus-Guided Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20022)
*Yaojie Zhang,Jianuo Huang,Junlong Ke,Yuhang Han,Yongji Long,Tianchen Zhao,Biqing Qi,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlexDraft 是一种无损推测解码框架，通过注意力调优、基于奖励的校准和灵活解码三种设计，在变批量大小下实现高效并行推测解码，解决了批量增大时吞吐量崩溃的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统顺序推测解码存在起草与验证相互等待、中间状态反复交换导致内存访问开销大的问题；现有并行推测解码方法在小批量下有效，但在大批量下因奖励令牌和接受长度的不确定性导致吞吐量崩溃。

Method: (1) 注意力调优：仅微调最后几层注意力投影器，冻结自回归路径以保持目标分布； (2) 基于奖励的校准：使用轻量级MLP基于已解析的奖励令牌校准草案logits； (3) 灵活解码：小批量时并行起草与验证，大批量时切换为顺序起草再验证，并根据草案置信度动态调整验证长度。

Result: FlexDraft在不同批量大小下都能保持高吞吐量，解决了大批量时并行推测解码吞吐量崩溃的问题，且无需昂贵的持续预训练，也无质量损失。

Conclusion: FlexDraft是一种适应变批量大小的无损推测解码框架，通过三种关键设计在保持生成质量的同时提升推理效率，具有实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates memory-bound LLM inference without quality degradation by using a fast drafter to propose multiple candidate tokens and the target model to verify them in parallel. However, conventional sequential speculative decoding suffers from mutual waiting between drafting and verification, and repeated exchange of intermediate states further increases memory access overhead. Parallel speculative decoding addresses this limitation by performing drafting and verification within a single target forward pass, allowing future drafts to be prepared while current candidates are being verified. Although effective at small batch sizes, existing parallel speculative decoding methods either require costly continual pretraining with quality degradation or suffer from low acceptance rates. More importantly, this paradigm inherently suffers from uncertainty in both the bonus token and the accepted length, leading to draft verification mismatch and causing throughput gains to collapse at large batch sizes. To address these limitations, we introduce FlexDraft, a lossless speculative decoding framework that flexibly adapts to varying batch sizes through three key designs. (1) Attention Tuning enables block diffusion drafting by tuning only the attention projectors of the final few layers on mask tokens, while keeping the autoregressive path frozen to preserve the target distribution and produce high quality drafts with minimal trainable parameters. (2) Bonus-guided Calibration uses a lightweight MLP conditioned on the resolved bonus token to calibrate draft logits, mitigating draft verification mismatch caused by bonus token uncertainty. (3) Flex Decoding dynamically switches between parallel draft and verify at small batch sizes and sequential draft then verify at large batch sizes, and adjusts verification length based on draft confidence to eliminate redundant computation.

</details>


### [147] [Mind Your Moras: Orthography-Aware Error Analysis of Neural Japanese Morphological Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20043)
*Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对日语过去式形态屈折进行了正字法感知的错误分析，将平假名视为编码形态音位区分的表征系统而非简单转录媒介。基于SIGMORPHON 2020和2023共享任务格式，评估两种字符级序列到序列架构。模型虽总体准确率高，但表现出系统性的、语言学上可解释的错误，集中在平假名的特定正字法属性上。引入包含七种主要失败模式的错误分类法。长音化相关错误占残差错误的75-80%，尤其出现在以元音e结尾且需要在过去式后缀前进行长音化的动词词干中。错误模式在架构和随机种子间高度一致，表明正字法表征、形态结构和数据频率效应之间存在稳健交互。结果强调了对形态复杂语言进行正字法感知评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经形态学模型在日语过去式屈折任务上虽表现出高准确率，但缺乏从正字法（尤其是平假名）角度深入分析模型错误的系统性研究。平假名不仅是转录媒介，其编码的形态音位区分可能影响模型泛化，因此有必要从正字法感知视角揭示模型的系统性失败模式。

Method: 采用两种字符级序列到序列架构，在SIGMORPHON 2020和2023共享任务格式的数据集上进行日语过去式形态屈折任务评估；构建包含七种主要失败模式的错误分类法，对模型输出进行定量和定性分析。

Result: 模型总体准确率高，但存在系统性的、语言学上可解释的错误；长音化相关错误占残差错误的75-80%，尤其集中出现在以元音e结尾且需长音化的动词词干上；错误模式在两种架构和不同随机种子间高度一致。

Conclusion: 正字法表征、形态结构和数据频率效应之间存在稳健交互，影响模型泛化能力；对形态复杂语言的神经模型评估必须采用正字法感知的方法，否则将忽略关键的系统性错误模式。

Abstract: We present an orthography-aware error analysis of Japanese past-tense morphological inflection, treating hiragana not merely as a transcriptional medium, but as a representational system encoding morphophonological distinctions that may influence model generalization. We evaluate two character-level sequence-to-sequence architectures on past-tense formation using datasets formatted according to the SIGMORPHON 2020 and 2023 shared task conventions. Despite high aggregate accuracy, models exhibit systematic, linguistically interpretable errors that cluster around specific orthographic properties of hiragana. We introduce a concise error taxonomy capturing seven primary failure modes and provide both quantitative and qualitative analyses. Gemination-related errors dominate residual failures, accounting for 75-80% of errors, particularly in verbs whose stems end in the vowel e and require gemination before the past-tense suffix. Error patterns remain highly consistent across architectures and random seeds, suggesting a robust interaction between orthographic representation, morphological structure, and data frequency effects in shaping model generalization. These results underscore the necessity of orthography-aware evaluation for understanding neural generalization in morphologically complex languages.

</details>


### [148] [Language Mutations Sustain the Persistences of Conspiracy Theories on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20050)
*Calvin Yixiang Cheng,Dorian Quelle,Scott A. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用三年间X平台上的阴谋论相关帖子数据，结合计算语言学和生存模型分析，发现语义突变越大的阴谋论主张存活时间越长。心理语言学特征（代词、社交指涉词、认知过程词、风险与健康相关词汇）以及行为者-行为-目标（AAT）类别的突变均与延长寿命相关。定性分析识别出两种主要突变模式：简化和同化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言突变如何影响阴谋论在社交媒体上的持续传播，理解其长期存活的机制。

Method: 使用三年期X平台阴谋论相关帖子数据集，应用计算语言学分析和生存模型（survival modelling），结合定性分析识别突变模式。

Result: 语义突变越大的阴谋论主张存活时间越长；心理语言学特征（代词、社交指涉词、认知过程词、风险与健康词汇）突变和AAT类别突变均与延长寿命相关；识别出简化和同化两种主要突变模式。

Conclusion: 语言突变是阴谋论在线上持续存在的重要因素，内容审核策略应考虑阴谋论主张的可变性，聚焦于能够应对其潜在变体的核心主张。

Abstract: This study investigates how language mutations affect the persistent diffusion of conspiracy theories on social media. Drawing on a three-year dataset of conspiracy-related posts from X, and applying computational linguistic analysis alongside survival modelling, we find that conspiracy claims with greater semantic mutations have substantially longer lifespans. Mutations in psycholinguistic properties, including pronouns, social reference words, cognitive process terms, risk- and health- related vocabularies, are associated with extended lifespans. Mutations in actor, action and target (AAT) categories are associated with longer lifespans as well. Qualitative analysis identifies two predominant mutation patterns: simplification and assimilation, at both linguistic and AAT structural levels. Taken together, the results advance our understanding of how language mutations contribute to conspiracy persistence online and shed lights on longitudinal content moderation strategies. We argue that content moderation should consider the mutability of conspiracy claims and focus on the core claims that can address their potential variations.

</details>


### [149] [PromptRad: Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Label Prompt-Tuning for Low-Resource Radiology Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20052)
*Ying-Jia Lin,Tzu-Chin Lo,Ping-Chien Li,Chi-Tung Cheng,Chien-Hung Liao,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PromptRad是一种知识增强的提示调优方法，用于低资源场景下的放射学报告多标签分类，仅需32个标注样本即可超越词典方法和传统微调基线，性能接近GPT-4但模型规模小得多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于规则的标注器难以处理临床报告中多样化的描述，而微调预训练语言模型需要大量标注数据，这在临床环境中常常难以获取。

Method: 提出PromptRad方法，将多标签分类重构为掩码语言建模任务，并从UMLS元同义词库中整合同义词到多词语言描述器中以丰富类别表示，无需额外的分类层即可微调PLM。

Result: 在肝脏CT报告上的实验表明，PromptRad仅用32个标注训练样本就优于基于词典和微调的基线方法，且在使用更小模型的情况下取得了与GPT-4竞争的性能。进一步分析显示PromptRad比现有方法更有效地捕获复杂否定模式。

Conclusion: PromptRad是数据稀缺临床场景下报告标注的有效解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automatic report labeling facilitates the identification of clinical findings from unstructured text and enables large-scale annotation for medical imaging research. Existing rule-based labelers struggle with the diverse descriptions in clinical reports, while fine-tuning pre-trained language models (PLMs) requires large amounts of labeled data that are often unavailable in clinical settings. In this paper, we propose PromptRad, a knowledge-enhanced multi-label \textbf{prompt}-tuning approach for \textbf{rad}iology report labeling under low-resource settings. PromptRad reformulates multi-label classification as masked language modeling and incorporates synonyms from the UMLS Metathesaurus into a multi-word verbalizer to enrich category representations. By fine-tuning the PLM without additional classification layers, PromptRad requires substantially less labeled data than conventional fine-tuning. Experiments on liver CT reports show that PromptRad outperforms dictionary-based and fine-tuning baselines with only 32 labeled training examples, and achieves competitive performance with GPT-4 despite using a much smaller model. Further analysis demonstrates that PromptRad captures complex negation patterns more effectively than existing methods, making it a promising solution for report labeling in data-scarce clinical scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ila-lab/PromptRad.

</details>


### [150] [Rewarding Beliefs, Not Actions: Consistency-Guided Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20061)
*Wenjie Tang,Minne Li,Sijie Huang,Liquan Xiao,Yuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReBel算法，通过显式建模信念状态并引入信念一致性监督和信念感知分组，解决了部分可观测环境下长程交互任务中的时序信用分配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上相比GRPO任务成功率提升最高20.4%，样本效率提升2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，智能体的信念会随时间漂移，而延迟奖励掩盖了中间决策的因果影响，导致长程交互任务中时序信用分配困难，现有RLVR方法难以有效处理这一问题。

Method: ReBel算法包含三个核心组件：(1) 显式结构化信念状态建模，用于汇总交互历史并引导策略学习；(2) 信念一致性监督，将预测信念与观测反馈之间的差异转化为密集的自监督信号，无需外部逐步骤标注；(3) 信念感知分组，在相似信念状态下比较轨迹，获得更稳健、方差更低的优势估计。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准测试上，ReBel相比GRPO任务成功率最高提升20.4个百分点，样本效率提升2.1倍。

Conclusion: 信念感知的自监督学习是部分可观测条件下可靠长程决策的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a promising paradigm for improving large language model (LLM) agents on long-horizon interactive tasks. However, in partially observable environments, incomplete observations cause agent beliefs to drift over time, while delayed rewards obscure the causal impact of intermediate decisions, exacerbating temporal credit assignment challenges. To address this, we propose ReBel (Reward Belief), a process-level reinforcement learning algorithm that explicitly models structured belief states to summarize interaction history and guide subsequent policy learning. ReBel introduces belief-consistency supervision, converting discrepancies between predicted beliefs and observed feedback into dense self-supervised signals without requiring external step-wise annotations or verifiers. It also employs belief-aware grouping to compare trajectories under similar belief states, yielding more robust and lower-variance advantage estimates. We evaluate ReBel on challenging long-horizon benchmarks, including ALFWorld and WebShop. ReBel improves task success by up to $20.4$ percentage points over the episode-level baseline GRPO and increases sample efficiency by $2.1\times$. These results suggest that belief-aware self-supervision is a promising direction for reliable long-horizon decision-making under partial observability. Code is available at: https://github.com/Fateyetian/Rebel.git.

</details>


### [151] [Text-to-SPARQL Generation with Reinforcement Learning: A GRPO-based Approach on DBLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20066)
*Jann Pfeifer,Debayan Banerjee,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用基于结果奖励的强化学习（GRPO）在小型指令微调语言模型（Qwen3-1.7B）上实现零样本Text-to-SPARQL生成，在DBLP-QuAD数据集上，GRPO显著提升零样本基线性能且泛化性良好，但监督微调（DoRA）整体准确率更高；消融实验表明执行奖励贡献最大，额外塑形收益有限，证明基于结果的强化学习在缺乏金查询标注时是可行的训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问答方法依赖大型模型或完整监督（金查询标注），本研究探索在缺乏令牌级监督的情况下，是否可以通过基于结果奖励的强化学习训练小型语言模型完成零样本Text-to-SPARQL生成。

Method: 采用Group-Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）应用于Qwen3-1.7B模型，在DBLP-QuAD数据集上使用自然语言问题结合实体关系符号提示进行训练；奖励基于执行反馈、结构约束和答案级奖励，并包含添加金查询塑形的变体；与未修改的零样本基线和监督DoRA微调基线在答案级准确率、执行准确率、类别得分及泛化性上进行比较。

Result: GRPO大幅优于零样本基线并展现出竞争性泛化能力；监督DoRA微调在同规模模型上取得更高的整体准确率；消融分析表明基于执行的奖励贡献了大部分增益，添加塑形奖励的额外收益有限。

Conclusion: 基于结果的强化学习在金查询不可用时是一种可行的训练策略，能够有效提升小型语言模型的零样本Text-to-SPARQL生成能力。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering seeks to translate natural language questions into executable queries over knowledge graphs, but existing approaches often rely on large models or full supervision in the form of gold query annotations. This study examines whether reinforcement learning with outcome-based rewards can train a small instruction-tuned language model to perform zero-shot Text-to-SPARQL generation in the scholarly domain. Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is applied to the Qwen3-1.7B model on DBLP-QuAD, using prompts that combine natural language questions with symbolic hints about entities and relations. Training relies on execution feedback, structural constraints, and answer-level rewards, with an additional variant that incorporates gold-query-based shaping. The resulting models are compared to the unmodified zero-shot baseline and to a supervised DoRA-finetuned baseline across answer-level accuracy, execution accuracy, category-wise scores, and generalization to held-out templates. GRPO substantially improves over the zero-shot baseline and exhibits competitive generalization, while supervised DoRA finetuning achieves higher overall accuracy on the same model scale. Ablation analyses indicate that execution-based rewards account for most gains, with additional shaping yielding limited additional benefit, suggesting that outcome-based reinforcement learning is a viable training strategy when gold queries are unavailable for token-level supervision.

</details>


### [152] [CopT: Contrastive On-Policy Thinking with Continuous Spaces for General and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20075)
*Dachuan Shi,Hanlin Zhu,Xiangchi Yuan,Wanjia Zhao,Kejing Xia,Wen Xiao,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CopT，一种反转CoT思考与回答顺序的新推理范式：先产生草稿答案，再基于该答案进行条件性思考用于反思修正。通过将连续嵌入作为推理时对比验证器评估答案可靠性，并动态控制草稿答案可见性，在不需额外训练的情况下，在数学、编程和智能体推理任务上最高提升23%准确率并减少57% token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CoT范式将思考视为回答的前提，导致即使模型能在扩展思考前识别出答案（即"表演性推理"行为），仍会延迟获取合理答案并产生不必要的token开销。

Method: (1) 反转推理流程：先获取草稿答案，再进行基于该答案的on-policy条件性思考用于反思修正；(2) 将连续嵌入作为推理时对比验证器：对比模型在离散token输入与连续嵌入输入下对相同生成token的支持度，得到序列级反向KL估计量用于评估答案可靠性；(3) 当答案不可靠时，使用第二个KL估计量动态控制草稿答案可见性，保留有用部分信息同时降低被不可靠内容误导的风险。

Result: 在数学、编程和智能体推理任务上，CopT相比标准CoT最高提升23%峰值准确率，在相当或更高准确率下最多减少57% token使用量。

Conclusion: CopT通过反转思考与回答的顺序，结合对比验证器进行可靠性评估，无需额外训练即可显著提升推理效率和准确性，为LLM推理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is a standard approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities from large language models (LLMs). However, the common CoT paradigm treats thinking as a prerequisite for answering, which can delay access to plausible answers and incur unnecessary token costs even when the model is able to identify an answer before extended thinking, a behavior known as performative reasoning. In this paper, we introduce CopT, a reformulated reasoning pipeline that reverses the usual order of thinking and answering. Instead of thinking before answering, CopT first elicits a draft answer and then invokes subsequent on-policy thinking conditioned on its own draft answer for reflection and correction. To assess whether the draft answer should be trusted, CopT recasts continuous embeddings as inference-time contrastive verifiers. Specifically, it contrasts the model's support for the same generated tokens under discrete-token inputs and continuous-embedding inputs, yielding a sequence-level reverse KL estimator for answer reliability. Our analysis shows that under certain assumptions, the expected estimate equals the mutual information between the unresolved latent state and the emitted answer token, explaining why it captures answer-relevant uncertainty rather than arbitrary uncertainty in the latent state. When the answer is deemed insufficiently reliable, CopT performs further on-policy thinking, where a second KL estimator dynamically controls draft-answer visibility, preserving useful partial information while reducing the risk of being misled by unreliable content. Across mathematics, coding, and agentic reasoning tasks, CopT improves peak accuracy by up to 23% and reduces token usage by up to 57% at comparable or higher accuracy, without any additional training. The code is available at https://github.com/sdc17/CopT.

</details>


### [153] [ThoughtTrace: Understanding User Thoughts in Real-World LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20087)
*Chuanyang Jin,Binze Li,Haopeng Xie,Cathy Mengying Fang,Tianjian Li,Shayne Longpre,Hongxiang Gu,Maximillian Chen,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ThoughtTrace是首个大规模数据集，收录了1,058名用户与20种语言模型交互时的自述想法（发送提示的原因及对助手回复的反应），包含2,155次对话、17,058轮次和10,174条想法标注。分析表明想法在语义上区别于消息、难以被前沿LLM推断、内容多样且与对话阶段相关。想法可用于改进用户行为预测和提供细粒度对齐信号以训练个性化助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI数据集仅记录用户说了什么（外在语言），而忽略了用户在想什么（内在认知状态）。为了深入理解人-AI交互背后的认知动态，需要一种能捕捉用户潜在目标、偏好和需求的新数据模态。

Method: 构建ThoughtTrace数据集：收集1,058名用户与20种语言模型进行多轮对话时的自报想法（包括发送提示的原因和对助手回复的反应），共2,155次对话、17,058轮次、10,174条想法标注。通过语义分析、LLM推断难度评估、内容分类和对话阶段关联分析来验证想法的独特性。

Result: ThoughtTrace捕捉了长程、主题多样的交互；想法在语义上显著区别于消息内容；前沿LLM难以从对话上下文中准确推断用户想法；想法内容多样且与对话阶段（如初始提示vs后续回复）密切相关。

Conclusion: 用户想法是一种有价值的新数据模态，能揭示人-AI交互背后的认知动态。想法数据可用于提升用户行为预测性能，并通过想法引导的重写为训练个性化助手提供细粒度对齐信号，从而构建更能理解并适应用户潜在目标、偏好和需求的AI系统。

Abstract: Conversational AI has now reached billions of users, yet existing datasets capture only what people say, not what they think. We introduce ThoughtTrace, the first large-scale dataset that pairs real-world multi-turn human--AI conversations with users' self-reported thoughts: their reasons for sending prompts and reactions to assistant responses. ThoughtTrace comprises 1,058 users, 2,155 conversations, 17,058 turns, and 10,174 thought annotations collected across 20 language models. Our analysis shows that ThoughtTrace captures long-horizon, topically diverse interactions, and that thoughts are semantically distinct from messages, difficult for frontier LLMs to infer from context, diverse in content, and tied to conversation stages. We further demonstrate the utility of thoughts for downstream modeling. First, thoughts improve user-behavior prediction as inference-time context. Second, thought-guided rewrites provide fine-grained alignment signals for training personalized assistants. Together, ThoughtTrace establishes user thoughts as a new data modality for studying the cognitive dynamics behind human--AI interaction and provides a foundation for building assistants that better understand and adapt to users' latent goals, preferences, and needs.

</details>


### [154] [MixRea: Benchmarking Explicit-Implicit Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20128)
*Yuanqing Cai,Ziyi Huang,Minhao Liu,Lixin Duan,Wen Li,Yanru Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"无意盲视"概念在LLM中的对应现象——模型在明确指令下仍会忽略关键上下文线索。为此构建MixRea基准（2246道选择题，9种推理类型），测试21个先进LLM后发现最佳模型(Gemini 2.5 Pro)仅42.8%一致性。提出PRCP提示方法通过恢复被忽略的因果联系来改善推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类认知中"无意盲视"理论启发，探究LLM是否因训练数据嵌入的人类注意力偏差而表现出类似局限——在明确任务指令下仍无法注意到细微但重要的上下文线索。

Method: 1) 构建MixRea基准：2246道选择题，覆盖9种推理类型，显式/隐式信息分布不同。2) 评估21个先进LLM。3) 提出PRCP提示方法，通过恢复被忽略的因果关联来改善推理。

Result: 最佳推理模型Gemini 2.5 Pro仅达42.8%一致性，揭示LLM普遍存在"无意盲视"现象。该局限在多种多源推理任务中持续存在。

Conclusion: LLM存在类似人类"无意盲视"的认知局限，需要开发更具认知对齐的模型来改进高利害决策中的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into high-stakes decision-making. Inspired by the theory of \emph{inattentional blindness} in human cognition, we investigate whether LLMs, trained on human-preferred corpora that embed attentional biases, exhibit a similar limitation: \emph{failing to attend to subtle yet important contextual cues under explicit task instructions}. To evaluate this, we introduce the task of \textbf{explicit-implicit reasoning} and present \textbf{MixRea}, a benchmark of 2,246 multiple-choice questions across 9 reasoning types with varying distributions of explicit and implicit information. Evaluation of 21 advanced LLMs shows that even the best-performing reasoning model (Gemini 2.5 Pro) achieves only 42.8\% consistency, revealing widespread inattentional blindness. To mitigate this, we propose \textbf{Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP)}, a prompting method that improves reasoning by recovering overlooked causal relations. Further analysis shows that this limitation persists across diverse multi-source reasoning tasks, highlighting the need for more cognitively aligned models.

</details>


### [155] [Less Back-and-Forth: A Comparative Study of Structured Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20149)
*Saurav Ghosh,Gabriella Polach,Abdou Sow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化提示设计对LLM响应质量和用户效率的影响，实验表明清单式提示（checklist-improved prompt）在四项任务（摘要、规划、解释、编码）和三个LLM系统（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok）中均取得最优效果，平均评分7.50/8，且token消耗最少，实现了最佳质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理开放式任务时，欠明确的提示词常导致回答质量低下并需要额外交互。本文旨在探索结构化提示设计是否能同时提升响应质量和减少用户投入。

Method: 对比三种提示条件：原始提示（raw prompt）、清单改进提示（checklist-improved prompt）和澄清提问提示（clarifying-question prompt）。涵盖四项任务类型（摘要、规划、解释、编码）和三个LLM系统（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok），使用统一评分标准（任务完成度、正确性、合规性、清晰度）进行评估。

Result: 清单改进提示的平均评分最高（7.50/8），高于原始提示（5.67）和澄清提问提示（6.67）。同时，清单提示的平均token消耗最少，实现了最佳的质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 简单的提示清单可以有效提升LLM响应质量，同时减少不必要的用户交互，为开放域任务提供了一种轻量级且高效的提示优化策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for open-ended tasks, but underspecified prompts can lead to low-quality answers and additional interaction. This paper studies whether structured prompt design improves response quality while reducing user effort. We compare three prompt conditions: a raw prompt, a checklist-improved prompt, and a clarifying-question prompt. We evaluate these conditions across four task types--summarization, planning, explanation, and coding--using three LLM systems: ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok. Each output is scored with a unified rubric covering task completion, correctness, compliance, and clarity. Checklist-improved prompts achieved the highest mean rubric score, 7.50 out of 8, compared with 5.67 for raw prompts and 6.67 for clarifying-question prompts. Checklist prompts also produced the best quality-effort tradeoff, using fewer average tokens than both raw and clarifying prompts. These results suggest that a simple prompt checklist can improve LLM responses while reducing unnecessary interaction.

</details>


### [156] [ClinSeekAgent: Automating Multimodal Evidence Seeking for Agentic Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20176)
*Juncheng Wu,Letian Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Haoqin Tu,Hardy Chen,Zijun Wang,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClinSeekAgent是一个自动化智能体框架，将临床决策支持从被动证据消费转变为主动证据获取。它通过查询知识库、浏览电子病历和调用医学影像工具来动态收集多模态证据，并能在推理时用于前沿LLM，也可作为训练管道将高质量智能体轨迹蒸馏到轻量开源模型。在文本和多模态临床任务上均显著提升性能，蒸馏后的ClinSeek-35B-A3B在AgentEHR-Bench上达到34.0平均F1，比基线提升+11.9点，接近Claude Opus 4.6水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM临床决策支持工作大多假设证据已被整理好直接交给模型，而真实临床工作流需要智能体主动从异构数据源中查找、迭代规划并综合多模态证据。现有范式是被动的证据消费，缺乏主动的证据获取能力。

Method: 提出ClinSeekAgent框架，包含：(1) 推理时智能体：给定临床查询和原始数据访问权限，通过查询医学知识库、浏览原始EHR、调用医学影像工具来收集证据，并在新信息出现时迭代优化假设，最终整合证据做出临床决策；(2) 训练时管道：将高质量智能体轨迹蒸馏到紧凑的开源模型中。同时构建ClinSeek-Bench基准测试，包含固定预选证据的Curated Input推理和原始临床数据的Automated Evidence-Seeking两种设置。

Result: 在文本EHR任务上，ClinSeekAgent将Claude Opus 4.6的F1从60.0提升到63.2，MiniMax M2.5从43.1提升到47.3，9个评估模型中7个获得正向风险预测提升。在多模态任务上，Claude Opus 4.6从47.5提升到62.6（+15.1），所有模型在三个CXR相关任务组均有提升。蒸馏后的ClinSeek-35B-A3B在AgentEHR-Bench上达到34.0平均F1，比Qwen3.5-35B-A3B基线提升+11.9点，接近Claude Opus 4.6。

Conclusion: ClinSeekAgent通过主动证据获取范式有效提升了LLM在临床决策支持中的表现，且能作为训练管道将能力蒸馏到轻量模型，推动了从被动消费到主动获取的范式转变。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and agentic systems have shown promise for clinical decision support, but existing works largely assume that evidence has already been curated and handed to the model. Real-world clinical workflows instead require agents to actively seek, iteratively plan, and synthesize multimodal evidence from heterogeneous sources. In this paper, we introduce ClinSeekAgent, an automated agentic framework for dynamic multimodal evidence seeking that shifts the paradigm from passive evidence consumption to active evidence acquisition. Given only a clinical query and access to raw data sources, ClinSeekAgent gathers evidence by querying medical knowledge bases, navigating raw EHRs, and invoking medical imaging tools; refines its hypotheses as new information emerges; and integrates the collected evidence into grounded clinical decisions. ClinSeekAgent serves both as an inference-time agent for frontier LLMs and as a training-time pipeline for distilling high-quality agent trajectories into compact open-source models. To validate its inference-time effectiveness, we construct ClinSeek-Bench, which pairs Curated Input reasoning from fixed pre-selected evidence with Automated Evidence-Seeking over raw clinical data. On text-only EHR tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 60.0 to 63.2 overall F1 and MiniMax M2.5 from 43.1 to 47.3, with positive risk-prediction gains in 7 out of 9 evaluated host models. On multimodal tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 47.5 to 62.6 (+15.1); all evaluated models improve across the three CXR-related task groups. We further validate ClinSeekAgent as a training pipeline by distilling agentic evidence-seeking trajectories into ClinSeek-35B-A3B, which achieves 34.0 average F1 on existing AgentEHR-Bench, improving over its Qwen3.5-35B-A3B baseline by +11.9 points and approaching Claude Opus 4.6.

</details>


### [157] [From Seeing to Thinking: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning Improves Post-Training of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20177)
*Juncheng Wu,Hardy Chen,Haoqin Tu,Xianfeng Tang,Freda Shi,Hui Liu,Hanqing Lu,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了VLM后训练中感知与推理的关系，提出将训练分解为视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理三个阶段。实验表明：视觉感知需要针对性优化，应作为基础先于推理训练，且RL优于SFT。分阶段训练相比合并训练一致提升感知和推理性能，感知提升后推理准确率提高1.5%且推理链缩短20.8%。该能力分阶段与难度分阶段正交，结合可产生加性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM强调长链思维推理，但其视觉任务性能主要受限于视觉感知能力而非推理能力。作者旨在解耦感知与推理，系统研究两者在VLM后训练中的相互作用关系。

Method: 将VLM能力分解为视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理三个独立训练阶段，分别使用专门训练数据。视觉感知使用RL而非SFT进行优化，采用分阶段训练策略，并与传统难度分阶段课程学习进行正交组合。

Result: 分阶段训练一致优于合并训练；推理准确率提升1.5%且推理链缩短20.8%；在WeMath和RealWorldQA上分别提升+5.2%和+3.7%；能力分阶段与难度分阶段正交结合产生加性收益。

Conclusion: 视觉感知是VLM性能提升的关键瓶颈，应通过分阶段训练先夯实感知基础，再优化推理能力。感知增强可减少对长推理链的依赖，该能力分阶段策略是一种新颖的课程学习维度。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) emphasize long chain-of-thought reasoning; yet, we find that their performance on visual tasks is primarily limited by a lack of visual perception as opposed to reasoning itself. In this work, we systematically study the interplay between perception and reasoning in VLM post-training by decomposing their capabilities into three separate training stages: visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning, incorporating specialized training data. We demonstrate that visual perception (a) requires targeted optimization with specialized data; (b) serves as a fundamental scaffold that should be solidified through staged training before refining visual reasoning; and (c) is more effectively learned via RL than caption-based SFT. Our experiments across multiple VLMs demonstrate that staged training consistently improves both visual perception and reasoning performance over merged training. Notably, models trained with our approach achieve 1.5% higher reasoning accuracy with 20.8% shorter reasoning traces, suggesting that superior perception reduces the need for excessive reasoning. Furthermore, we show that this capability-based staging represents a new curriculum dimension orthogonal to traditional difficulty-based curricula, and combining both yields further additive gains. Our staged-training models achieve superior performance among open-weight VLMs, establishing advanced results on several visual math and perception (e.g., +5.2% on WeMath and +3.7% on RealWorldQA) tasks compared with the base counterpart.

</details>


### [158] [TIDE: Efficient and Lossless MoE Diffusion LLM Inference with I/O-aware Expert Offload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20179)
*Zhiben Chen,Youpeng Zhao,Yang Sui,Jun Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TIDE是一个面向扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的高效推理系统，利用块内扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性，通过基于间隔的I/O感知专家刷新策略和数学规划求解最优调度，实现无损、免训练的推理加速，在单GPU-CPU系统上相较基线方法取得高达1.4-1.5倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）随着混合专家（MoE）架构的扩展，在资源受限设备上的部署面临挑战，现有基于自回归（AR）的方法存在严重的I/O开销或计算瓶颈。

Method: 利用块内扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性，提出基于间隔的I/O感知专家刷新策略；将推理调度形式化为数学规划问题，求解最优更新间隔以最小化I/O流量和CPU计算。

Result: 在单GPU-CPU系统上，TIDE在LLaDA2.0-mini和LLaDA2.0-flash模型上分别实现了相较于基线方法最高1.4倍和1.5倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: TIDE是一种无损、免训练的优化方法，为dLLM推理提供了“免费午餐”式的加速效果，有效解决了MoE架构dLLM在资源受限设备上的部署难题。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, offering better hardware utilization and bidirectional context through parallel block-level decoding. However, as dLLMs continue to scale up with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, their deployment on resource-constrained devices remains an open challenge. Existing AR-based methods often incur either prohibitive I/O overhead or significant compute bottlenecks. In this work, we propose TIDE, a novel resource-efficient inference system that leverages the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block. Specifically, we leverage the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block and introduce an interval-based expert refresh strategy that updates the expert placement in an I/O-aware fashion. To ensure optimal performance, we formulate the inference scheduling as a mathematical programming problem, solving for the optimal interval that minimizes I/O traffic and CPU computation. Most importantly, TIDE is a lossless optimization that requires no model training, providing a "free lunch" acceleration for dLLM inference. In a single GPU-CPU system, we demonstrate that TIDE achieves up to 1.4$\times$ and 1.5$\times$ throughput improvements over prior baselines on LLaDA2.0-mini and LLaDA2.0-flash models, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Generative and isoparametric geometric modeling of large-scale and multiscale microstructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18894)
*Guoyue Luo,Yuntao Ma,Qiang Zou*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出ExVCC方法，一种基于扩展体积Catmull-Clark样条的按需生成式几何建模方法，通过局部样条细化、形状编码和跨尺度等参表示，实现大规模微观结构的高效紧凑表示与多尺度关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造向更高分辨率和更大构建体积发展时，微观结构需在更大物理域上包含更精细的几何特征，现有显式表示内存开销大，隐式表示仅对解析/周期结构紧凑，缺乏可扩展的紧凑表示方法。

Method: 1) 提出ExVCC（扩展体积Catmull-Clark样条），支持超越张量积拓扑的局部样条细化；2) 设计新的形状编码方案和细化规则，通过按需层次化细化实现大规模几何细节的紧凑编码与局部化评估；3) 提出等参表示，各尺度细节在同一参数域上用同族样条基定义，将细化层次统一为编码、生成和跨尺度关联框架。

Result: 通过一系列示例和对比验证，该方法能有效表示大规模微观结构，支持按需生成和跨尺度几何修改的自动传播。

Conclusion: ExVCC为大规模微观结构建模提供了一种紧凑、可扩展且支持跨尺度一致性的新几何建模范式。

Abstract: As additive manufacturing advances toward higher printing resolution and larger build volumes, microstructures can be designed with finer geometric features over larger physical domains. This trend poses a fundamental challenge for geometric modeling: massive geometric details must be represented compactly, while their associations across scales must be maintained consistently.Existing methods cannot scale well to this requirement. Explicit representations suffer from prohibitive memory cost, and implicit representations remain compact only when microstructures admit analytic, periodic, or otherwise concise procedural descriptions. This paper proposes a new geometric modeling method that treats microstructure modeling as an on-demand generative process, rather than requiring the full instantiation of all geometric details. We first develop ExVCC, an extended volumetric Catmull-Clark spline representation that enables local spline refinement to go beyond tensor-product topology. Built on ExVCC, we introduce new shape-coding schemes and refinement rules that compactly encode large-scale geometric details and enable their localized evaluation through on-demand hierarchical refinement. To model geometric details across scales, we further propose an isoparametric representation in which details across scales are defined over a shared parametric domain using the same family of spline bases of ExVCC. This formulation turns the ExVCC's spline refinement hierarchy into a common framework for geometry encoding, on-demand generation, and cross-scale association, allowing geometric modifications to propagate automatically across scales. The effectiveness of the proposed method is demonstrated through a series of examples and comparisons.

</details>


### [160] [Spatially Accelerated Winding Numbers for Curved Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19200)
*Jacob Spainhour,Brad Whitlock,Kenneth Weiss*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于包围体层次结构（BVH）和自适应细分策略的方法，用于在NURBS曲线（2D）和裁剪NURBS曲面（3D）上高效计算广义缠绕数（GWN），在保持边界附近精度的同时实现亚线性复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的快速GWN方法仅适用于离散输入（如三角网格和点云），直接对参数曲线和曲面进行GWN评估计算成本高昂，且若将现有方法直接应用于曲线/曲面输入会在边界附近引入包含查询错误。

Method: 使用包围体层次结构（BVH）存储预计算的矩数据；对远处图元簇采用近似计算（基于矩），对近处NURBS图元直接评估；在预处理阶段对NURBS图元采用自适应细分策略以创建更好的空间划分。

Result: 在2D和3D数据集上验证了方法的性能和精度，实现了亚线性复杂度，同时保持了与直接评估相同的包含决策精度。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将快速GWN评估扩展到NURBS曲线和曲面输入，兼顾了计算效率与边界附近的几何特征保持。

Abstract: The generalized winding number (GWN) is a scalar field that supports robust containment queries on curved geometry, including non-watertight, overlapping, and nested boundary representations. While queries can be easily parallelized over samples, direct evaluation on parametric curves and surfaces remains costly for large and complex models. Fast, state-of-the-art GWN approaches leverage a spatial index to approximate the GWN, typically coupled with a Taylor expansion which approximates the GWN contribution for far clusters of geometric primitives. However, such methods operate only on discrete inputs such as triangle meshes and point clouds, and would introduce containment errors near boundaries if applied to curved input. We extend support for fast GWN evaluation over arbitrary collections of NURBS curves in 2D and trimmed NURBS patches in 3D via a Bounding Volume Hierarchy that stores efficiently precomputed moment data in the hierarchy nodes. When querying the hierarchy, approximations for far clusters are used alongside direct evaluation for nearby NURBS primitives, achieving sub-linear complexity while preserving the geometric features in the vicinity of the query point. Central to our performance improvements is an adaptive subdivision strategy for NURBS primitives during a preprocessing phase, creating better spatial partitions while retaining the same accuracy for containment decisions as a direct evaluation. We demonstrate the performance and accuracy of our approach across a large collection of 2D and 3D datasets.

</details>


### [161] [CompoSE: Compositional Synthesis and Editing of 3D Shapes via Part-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19350)
*Habib Slim,Shariq Farooq Bhat,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yifan Wang,Mike Roberts*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: CompoSE是一种基于扩散Transformer架构的组合式3D形状合成与编辑方法，通过部件感知控制，利用粗粒度几何基元（如包围盒）作为输入，生成支持局部精细编辑的部件分离3D对象，无需部件级文本提示即可推断部件语义和对称性，在引导合成任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机图形学中创建和编辑高质量3D内容仍是一个核心挑战，现有方法在部件级细粒度编辑方面能力不足。

Method: 采用扩散Transformer架构，交替进行部件本地处理和跨部件全局上下文信息聚合；提出新颖的条件控制技术确保对用户输入的强依从性；从粗粒度布局指导中自动学习部件语义和对称性。

Result: 在客观指标和基于LLM的评估中，该方法在引导合成任务上显著优于现有方法，支持上下文感知的部件替换、添加、删除及风格保持的大小调整等编辑操作。

Conclusion: CompoSE实现了强大的部件级编辑能力，无需部件级文本提示即可完成高质量的3D内容合成与编辑。

Abstract: Creating and editing high-quality 3D content remains a central challenge in computer graphics. We address this challenge by introducing CompoSE, a novel method for Compositional Synthesis and Editing of 3D shapes via part-aware control. Our method takes as input a set of coarse geometric primitives (e.g., bounding boxes) that represent distinct object parts arranged in a particular spatial configuration, and synthesizes as output part-separated 3D objects that support localized granular (i.e., compositional) editing of individual parts. The key insight that enables our method is our use of a diffusion transformer architecture that alternates between processing each part locally and aggregating contextual information across parts globally, and features a novel conditioning technique that ensures strong adherence to the user's input. Importantly, our method learns to infer part semantics and symmetries directly from the user's coarse layout guidance, and does not require part-level text prompts. We demonstrate that our method enables powerful part-level editing capabilities, including context-aware substitution, addition, deletion, and style-preserving resizing operations. We show through extensive experiments that our method significantly outperforms existing approaches on guided synthesis, as measured by objective metrics and LLM-based evaluations.

</details>


### [162] [Skinned Motion Retargeting with Spatially Adaptive Interaction Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19355)
*Soojin Choi,Seokhyeon Hong,Chaelin Kim,Junghyun Nam,Junhyuk Jeon,Junyong Noh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于空间自适应锚点的几何感知运动重定向框架，通过Transformer锚点细化策略和可微软投影实现锚点动态重定位，结合图自编码器预测骨架运动，采用交替训练策略，在保持交互语义（如自接触和近体邻近性）方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何感知方法依赖预定义对应区域的静态锚点，当目标角色具有夸张身体比例时效果不佳，难以保持交互语义（如自接触和近体邻近性）。

Method: 1) 基于Transformer的锚点细化策略，动态将锚点重定位到目标角色的可达区域；2) 可微软投影约束锚点保持在目标角色几何表面；3) 图自编码器基于适应后的锚点预测目标骨架运动；4) 交替训练策略促进锚点适应与运动重定向的联合优化。

Result: 在多种角色几何体上的广泛评估表明，该方法在保持交互保真度方面优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过空间自适应锚点机制，有效解决了跨不同体型角色运动重定向中交互语义保持的难题，显著提升了对夸张身体比例的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retargeting motion across characters with varying body shapes while preserving interaction semantics, such as self-contact and near-body proximity, remains a challenging problem. While recent geometry-aware approaches address this by maintaining spatial relationships between predefined corresponding regions, their reliance on static correspondences often struggles when the target character exhibits exaggerated body proportions. In this paper, we present a geometry-aware motion retargeting framework that preserves interaction semantics by performing proximity matching over spatially adaptive anchors. Unlike prior methods with static anchor definitions, the proposed method dynamically repositions anchors to reachable regions on the target character. This is achieved via a Transformer-based anchor refinement strategy that predicts anchor displacements and constrains the translated anchors to remain on the target character geometry through differentiable soft projection. By incorporating pose-dependent spatial structures from the source character, the adapted anchors provide structurally coherent guidance for interaction-aware retargeting. Conditioned on these anchors, a graph-based autoencoder predicts target skeletal motion that preserves the spatial configuration of the source. To encourage task-aligned optimization between anchor adaptation and motion retargeting, we adopt an alternating training scheme in which each module is optimized in turn. Through extensive evaluations, we demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in preserving interaction fidelity across diverse character geometries.

</details>


### [163] [AnchorFlow: Editable SVG Reconstruction via Sparse Anchor Point Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19551)
*Mengnan Jiang,Christian Franke,Michele Franco Adesso,Antonio Haas,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: AnchorFlow通过稀疏锚点场建模路径级锚点放置，在SVG重建中实现了保真度与可编辑性的良好平衡，显著降低了可编辑复杂度同时保持了竞争力的光栅保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到SVG重建方法在向量结构参数化（路径数量、锚点密度）上面临结构性权衡：高保真方法依赖密集路径或曲线，而紧凑SVG生成可能偏离输入几何，特别是在局部光栅证据不完善时会产生冗余锚点和碎片化结构。

Method: 提出AnchorFlow框架：从光栅图像提取路径状前景组件，为每个组件预测图像条件化的稀疏锚点场并解析为有序贝塞尔路径，通过渲染引导反馈修正局部结构错误，最终组装和优化为完整SVG。

Result: 在孤立路径和完整图像上的实验表明，AnchorFlow实现了保真度与可编辑性的有利平衡，显著降低了可编辑复杂度同时保持了竞争力的光栅保真度。

Conclusion: 锚点放置是解决SVG重建中保真度-可编辑性权衡的关键层面，AnchorFlow通过稀疏锚点场建模有效解决了这一权衡问题。

Abstract: Image-to-SVG reconstruction aims to produce vector graphics that are faithful to raster inputs and easy to edit. Existing methods face a structural trade-off in how vector structure is parameterized, including how many paths represent an image and how many anchor points define each path. High-fidelity methods often rely on many paths or densely parameterized curves, whereas overly compact SVG generation may deviate from the input geometry. This issue becomes more pronounced when local raster evidence is imperfect, where boundary-following reconstruction can introduce redundant anchors and fragmented structures. We argue that this trade-off should be addressed at the level of anchor placement, since anchors on Bezier curves define local path structure and strongly affect both accuracy and editability. We propose AnchorFlow, an editable SVG reconstruction framework that models path-level anchor placement with sparse anchor point fields. Given path-like foreground components extracted from a raster image, AnchorFlow predicts an image-conditioned sparse anchor field for each component and resolves it into an ordered Bezier path. Rendering-guided feedback then corrects local structural errors before re-resolution. The recovered paths are then assembled and optimized into the final SVG. Experiments on isolated paths and full images show that AnchorFlow achieves a favorable fidelity-editability trade-off, substantially reducing editable complexity while preserving competitive raster fidelity.

</details>


### [164] [Decentralized Direct Volume Rendering: A Browser-Native GPU Architecture for MRI Digital Twins in Resource-Constrained Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19737)
*Oserebameh Augustine Beckley*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于客户端WebGPU的去中心化架构，在低功耗集成GPU上实现高保真解剖学数字孪生的实时渲染与交互，无需服务器端渲染或深度学习依赖，TTFP低于920ms，帧率稳定在82FPS以上，为资源受限环境下的临床数字孪生推广提供了可扩展的低成本方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字孪生技术依赖服务器端渲染或昂贵本地工作站，部署门槛高，尤其在资源受限环境中难以推广。亟需一种低成本、去中心化的方案，使高保真解剖学数字孪生可广泛用于手术规划和个性化医疗。

Method: 采用客户端WebGPU架构，在低功耗集成边缘GPU上直接执行确定性单遍光线行进（single-pass raymarching）和形态学梯度计算，通过统一缓冲区实现零延迟组织参数操控，绕过了传统服务器端渲染管线。

Result: 系统首次像素时间低于920ms，交互帧率稳定在82FPS以上，实现了无需深度学习或外部计算依赖的浏览器原生3D医学模拟。

Conclusion: 该架构证明了复杂3D医学模拟可在浏览器端本地运行，为资源受限环境下医疗数字孪生的规模化临床采纳提供了可负担、可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Digital Twin (DT) technology holds immense potential for surgical planning and personalized medicine. However, generating interactive, patient-specific anatomical twins currently relies on computationally heavy Server-Side Rendering (SSR) or expensive local workstations, creating significant barriers to deployment, especially in resource-constrained settings (RCS). This paper presents a decentralized, client-side WebGPU architecture that democratizes access to high-fidelity anatomical Digital Twins. By bypassing standard server-side rendering pipelines, the framework executes deterministic single-pass raymarching and morphological gradient calculations directly on low-cost integrated edge GPUs. Eliminating the network latency inherent to cloud-rendered solutions, the system achieves a Time to First Pixel (TTFP) of under 920.0ms and maintains stable interactivity at >= 82.0 FPS. Continuous Interaction Fidelity is maintained via uniform buffers, enabling zero-latency manipulation of tissue parameters for dynamic clinical decision-making. By proving that complex 3D medical simulations of patient-specific MRI scan can be executed natively in the browser without deep learning or external computational dependencies, this architecture provides a scalable, affordable foundation for the widespread clinical adoption of healthcare Digital Twins.

</details>


### [165] [GLUT: 3D Gaussian Lookup Table for Continuous Color Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19889)
*Danna Xue,David Serrano-Lozano,Shaolin Su,Javier Vazquez-Corral*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出Gaussian LUT (GLUT)，用一组可学习的3D高斯基元替代传统3D LUT的固定网格表示，实现连续且显式的色彩变换建模，在保持高精度和低存储的同时，支持可解释性与局部颜色编辑，并进一步提出条件生成器CGLUT实现多风格LUT的统一生成与平滑融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D LUT依赖离散RGB网格，面临容量-内存权衡，存储大量LUT时成本高昂；现有隐式神经表示虽提升可扩展性，但黑盒性质缺乏可解释性，难以进行直观的局部编辑。

Method: 提出GLUT，用一组可学习的3D高斯基元作为连续显式颜色表示，避免固定分辨率网格；进一步提出CGLUT紧凑条件生成器，从单一框架预测多个LUT实例的参数，支持风格混合与平滑控制。

Result: 实验表明，GLUT在准确性和效率上均优于先前的神经LUT表示方法，同时具备更好的可解释性和交互控制能力。

Conclusion: GLUT通过连续显式建模在精度、存储、可解释性和编辑灵活性之间取得更优平衡，为色彩映射任务提供了一种高效且用户友好的新范式。

Abstract: 3D Lookup Tables (3D LUTs) are widely used for color mapping, but their grid-based representation requires discretizing the RGB space, leading to a capacity-memory trade-off that becomes prohibitive when storing large numbers of LUTs. Recent approaches adopt implicit neural representations to improve scalability, yet their black-box nature limits interpretability and hinders intuitive, localized editing. In this paper, we propose Gaussian LUT (GLUT), a continuous and explicit color representation that models color transformations using a set of learnable 3D Gaussian primitives. By avoiding fixed-resolution grids, GLUT achieves flexible representational capacity while maintaining a compact memory footprint. Its explicit, spatially localized formulation further enables both accurate modeling and interpretability. Building on this representation, we introduce a compact conditional generator (CGLUT) that predicts GLUT parameters for multiple LUT instances, encoding diverse color styles in a single framework to enable smooth and controllable LUT style blending. Moreover, GLUT supports efficient, user-friendly editing by allowing localized adjustments to specific color regions without global retraining. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach outperforms prior neural LUT representations in both accuracy and efficiency, while offering improved interpretability and interactive control.

</details>


### [166] [PiG-Avatar: Hierarchical Neural-Field-Guided Gaussian Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20185)
*Julian Kaltheuner,Jan Spindler,Sina Kitz,Patrick Stotko,Reinhard Klein*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: PiG-Avatar 提出了一种将高斯点云锚定在连续神经场控制的体积规范空间中的方法，将人体参数模型仅用于运动传输，从而解耦了表示空间与模板拓扑，能够自然捕获复杂的衣物几何和多层表面，在多个基准上实现实时、高质量的渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的人体化身方法通常将几何参数化在人体模板表面，导致表示空间与模板变形空间纠缠，限制了对手部、身体外及非刚性衣物几何的捕获能力。

Method: 1) 使用参数化人体模型仅用于运动传输，将高斯点锚定在由连续神经场控制的体积规范空间中；2) 通过3D重心锚点传输维持运动一致性，允许锚点自由偏离模板表面；3) 引入双层级空间一致性优化：Sobolev预条件的神经场更新 + KNN预条件的规范锚点几何优化，实现锚点密度的自组织。

Result: 在复杂衣物和挑战性非刚性运动的基准上达到SOTA渲染质量，能鲁棒地泛化到不完美的人体模型初始化，并在所有细节层级上实现实时渲染。

Conclusion: 通过解耦表示空间与模板拓扑，PiG-Avatar能够自然地重建复杂的衣物几何和多层表面，支持多层级细节的分层级重建，是一种高效且高质量的人体化身方法。

Abstract: Existing Gaussian avatar methods typically parameterize geometry on a body-template surface, which entangles the avatar's representation space with the template's deformation space and limits the capture of layered, off-body, and non-rigid clothing geometry. We present PiG-Avatar, which addresses this limitation by using the parametric body model solely for kinematic transport, while representing the avatar as Gaussians anchored in a volumetric canonical space governed by a continuous neural field. This decouples representation from template topology, avoiding the geometric constraints of surface-based parameterizations. Kinematic coherence is maintained through 3D barycentric anchor transport, which guides motion without constraining geometry and allows anchors to deviate freely from the template surface, yielding dense, stable temporal surface correspondences by construction. To make this unconstrained formulation tractable, we introduce dual-level spatially coherent optimization, combining Sobolev-preconditioned neural-field updates with a novel KNN-based preconditioning of canonical anchor geometry. Together, these mechanisms induce an emergent self-organization of anchor density: anchors migrate toward regions of high curvature, appearance variation, and non-coherent motion without explicit heuristics. As a result, complex clothing geometry and layered surfaces emerge as natural, high-fidelity outputs. This single representation further supports hierarchical reconstruction across multiple levels of detail, with coarse-level supervision propagating to finer levels through the shared field and coupled anchor graph. On established benchmarks featuring subjects with complex clothing and challenging non-rigid motion, PiG-Avatar achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality, generalizes robustly to imperfect body model initialization, and renders in real time across all detail levels.

</details>


### [167] [An Efficient Multilevel Preconditioned Nonlinear Conjugate Gradient Method for Incremental Potential Contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19892)
*Yu Zhang,Xing Shen,Kemeng Huang,Wei Chen,Yin Yang,Taku Komura,Tiantian Liu,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAS-PNCG 方法，通过稀疏输入 Woodbury 更新算法实现分层预条件子的高效维护，结合 Hessian 感知的二维子空间最小化和保守 CCD 方法，在接触丰富的仿真场景中显著加速了增量势接触（IPC）求解，相比基于 Newton-PCG 的 GIPC 和 StiffGIPC 分别实现了最高 5.66 倍和 2.07 倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPC 虽然能保证无相交仿真，但 Newton 法所需的 Hessian 组装和线性求解计算成本高昂；PNCG 虽避免 Hessian 组装，但在刚性和接触丰富场景下因缺乏有效预条件子而收敛缓慢（Jacobi 预条件子无法捕捉全局耦合，而 MAS 等层次化预条件子在每个非线性迭代中重建的计算代价过高）。

Method: 1) 提出稀疏输入 Woodbury 更新算法，增量地调整细层 MAS 组件以适应快速演化的接触集，避免完全重建预条件子；2) 用 Hessian 感知的二维子空间最小化替代启发式 PNCG 搜索方向，最优结合预条件梯度和前一步方向；3) 采用快速逐子域保守 CCD 方法，确保无穿透轨迹同时避免过于保守的全局步长。

Result: MAS-PNCG 在多个接触丰富场景中优于使用 MAS 预条件的 Newton-PCG 求解器 GIPC 和 StiffGIPC，分别实现最高 5.66 倍和 2.07 倍的加速。

Conclusion: 通过创新的稀疏更新算法解决了层次化预条件子在非线性优化中的维护瓶颈，使得 PNCG 在接触丰富的刚体仿真中也能高效收敛，为 IPC 框架下的高效仿真提供了新途径。

Abstract: Incremental Potential Contact (IPC) guarantees intersection-free simulation but suffers from high computational costs due to the expensive Hessian assembly and linear solves required by Newton's method. While Preconditioned Nonlinear Conjugate Gradient (PNCG) avoids Hessian assembly, it has historically struggled with poor convergence in stiff, contact-rich scenarios due to the lack of effective preconditioners; simple Jacobi preconditioners fail to capture the global coupling, while advanced hierarchy-based preconditioners like Multilevel Additive Schwarz (MAS) are computationally prohibitive to rebuild at every nonlinear iteration. We present MAS-PNCG, a method that unlocks the power of hierarchical preconditioning for nonlinear optimization. Our key technical innovation is a Sparse-Input Woodbury update algorithm that incrementally adapts the fine-level MAS components to rapidly evolving contact sets. This bypasses the need for full preconditioner rebuilds, reducing maintenance cost to near-zero while capturing the complex spectral properties of the contact system. Furthermore, we replace heuristic PNCG search directions with a Hessian-aware 2D subspace minimization that optimally combines the preconditioned gradient and previous direction. We also apply a fast per-subdomain conservative CCD method that ensures penetration-free trajectories while avoiding overly restrictive global step sizes. Experiments demonstrate that our MAS-PNCG outperforms state-of-the-art Newton-PCG solvers, GIPC and StiffGIPC, both preconditioned with MAS up to 5.66$\times$ and 2.07$\times$ respectively.

</details>


### [168] [Matérn Noise for Triangulation-Agnostic Flow Matching on Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19305)
*Tianshu Kuai,Arman Maesumi,Daniel Ritchie,Noam Aigerman*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流匹配（Flow Matching）的三角网格信号生成方法，该方法与网格的三角剖分无关（triangulation-agnostic）。通过使用Matérn过程作为噪声分布并适配PoissonNet作为去噪器，实现了高质量、多样化的网格信号生成，在超过百万三角面的网格上显著超越了现有技术水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网格上的信号生成方法通常依赖于特定的三角剖分，导致训练好的模型无法直接应用于不同拓扑结构的网格。本文旨在设计一种与三角剖分无关的生成框架，使得模型能够在多种网格上泛化。

Method: 1）将流匹配（Flow Matching）范式适配到三角网格的三角剖分无关场景；2）理论上提出了一种三角剖分无关的噪声分布定义（基于谱分析），并证明Matérn过程的离散化满足该性质；3）使用PoissonNet作为去噪器，在梯度域学习网格信号。

Result: 在弹性静息状态采样和人形姿态生成等复杂任务上，所提方法在超过百万三角面的网格上生成了高度真实的结果，在质量和多样性上显著超过现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将流匹配推广到三角剖分无关的网格信号生成场景，Matérn过程作为噪声模型兼顾了理论性质和计算效率，为大规模网格生成任务提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper tackles the task of learning to generate signals over triangle meshes in a triangulation-agnostic manner, meaning the trained model can be applied to different meshes and triangulations effectively. Practically, the paper adapts the flow matching (FM) paradigm to a mesh-based, triangulation-agnostic setting. Theoretically, it proposes a specific noise distribution which is triangulation agnostic, to be used inside the FM model's denoising process. While noise distributions are usually trivial to devise for, e.g., images, devising a triangulation-agnostic distribution proves to be a much more difficult task. We formulate a mathematical definition of triangulation agnosticism of distributions, via their spectrum. We then show that a discretization of a specific Gaussian random field called a Matérn process holds these desired properties, and provides a simple and efficient sampling algorithm. We use it as our noise model, and adapt FM to the triangulation-agnostic setting by using a state-of-the-art approach for learning signals on meshes in the gradient domain -- PoissonNet -- as the denoiser. We conduct experiments on elaborate tasks such as sampling elastic rest states, and generating poses of humanoids. Our method is shown to be capable of producing highly realistic results for meshes of over one million triangles, significantly exceeding the state-of-the-art in quality and diversity.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
*Shiqiang Wang,Herbert Woisetschläger,Hans Arno Jacobsen,Mingyue Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据探针"概念——通过从随机过程中生成合成序列作为可控实验工具，系统研究数据特征如何影响大语言模型在训练、微调、对齐、上下文学习等不同阶段的行为，以超越当前依赖经验性启发式的数据处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对什么数据在LLM工作流中有效及其原因的理解仍是一个开放问题，现有方法依赖大规模实验获取经验性启发式，计算密集且缺乏原则性理解。

Method: 倡导开发从适当定义的随机过程中生成合成序列的系统方法论（称为"数据探针"），通过观察LLM在这些探针上的行为，系统研究数据特征对模型性能、泛化性和鲁棒性的影响。这些探针序列具有可借助典型集等理论概念分析的统计特性。

Result: 数据探针能够使研究者系统性地开展关于数据特征如何影响模型性能、泛化性和鲁棒性的研究，其统计特性可通过典型集等理论概念进行描述。

Conclusion: 数据探针方法为超越经验性启发式、揭示数据在LLM训练和推理中作用的基础性洞察提供了途径。

Abstract: Data is fundamental to large language models (LLMs). However, understanding of what makes certain data useful for different stages of an LLM workflow, including training, tuning, alignment, in-context learning, etc., and why, remains an open question. Current approaches rely heavily on extensive experimentation with large public datasets to obtain empirical heuristics for data filtering and dataset construction. These approaches are compute intensive and lack a principled way of understanding the essence of how specific data characteristics drive LLM behavior. In this position paper, we advocate for the need of developing systematic methodologies for generating synthetic sequences from appropriately defined random processes, with the goal that these sequences can reveal useful characteristics when they are used in one or multiple stages of the LLM workflow. We refer to such sequences as data probes. By observing LLM behavior on data probes, researchers can systematically conduct studies on how data characteristics influence model performance, generalization, and robustness. The probing sequences exhibit statistical properties that can be viewed using theoretical concepts, such as typical sets, which are generalized to describe the behaviors of LLMs. This data-probe approach provides a pathway for uncovering foundational insights into the role of data in LLM training and inference, beyond empirical heuristics.

</details>


### [170] [Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
*Yao Fehlis,Benjamin Bengfort,Zhangzhang Si,Vahid Eyorokon,Prema Roman,Patrick Deziel,Devon Slonaker,Steve Veldman,Ben Johnson,Joyce Rigelo,Michael Wharton,Steve Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种微服务架构，将分类、OCR和LLM结构化字段提取等多个模型封装成管道，支持每小时处理数千份多页文档，并分享了生产环境中的设计决策和性能发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界研究多聚焦于文档理解新模型，导致模型定义与生产级运行之间存在巨大鸿沟，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 采用微服务架构，管道包含分类模型、OCR和大语言模型结构化字段提取；设计决策包括混合分类、GPU推理与CPU编排分离、异步IO处理、独立水平扩展策略，并通过批次性能分析进行优化。

Result: 两个关键发现：OCR（而非语言模型解析）主导端到端延迟；系统饱和度由共享GPU推理容量决定，而非工作线程数量。

Conclusion: 为从业者提供了超越基准测试、实现文档理解系统生产化部署的具体架构模式。

Abstract: Academic research tends to focus on new models for document understanding creating a wide gap in the literature between model definition and running models at production scale. To close that gap, we present a microservice architecture that encapsulates pipelines of multiple models for classification, optical character recognition (OCR), and large language model structured field extraction as well as our experience running this pipeline on thousands of multi-page documents per hour. We describe our primary design decisions, including a hybrid classification, separation of GPU-bound inference from CPU-bound orchestration, use of asynchronous processing for the many IO-bound operations in the pipeline, and an independent, horizontal scaling strategy. Using batch profiling, we identified two surprising qualitative findings that shape production deployments: OCR, not language-model parsing, dominates end-to-end latency, and the system saturates at a concurrency determined by shared GPU-inference capacity rather than worker count. Our goal is to provide practitioners with concrete architectural patterns for building document understanding systems that work beyond the benchmark; effectively operationalizing models in production.

</details>


### [171] [Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
*Anis Radianis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LBW-Guard，一个运行在AdamW优化器之上的有界自主训练控制层，通过观察训练遥测并应用有界控制来提升大语言模型训练的稳定性，在Qwen2.5系列模型上实现困惑度降低18.7%和1.10倍加速，尤其在激进学习率场景下保持可训练性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型训练在学习率激进、规模扩大和运行时压力下日益暴露于不稳定性、训练退化以及算力浪费问题，现有方法如梯度裁剪无法有效解决此类问题，亟需一种新的训练稳定性保障机制。

Method: LBW-Guard作为一个位于AdamW之上的有界自主训练控制治理层，不替换优化器更新规则，而是通过观察训练遥测数据、解释不稳定敏感区域，并在保持固定训练目标的前提下对优化器执行施加有界控制。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B参考设置中，最终困惑度从13.21降至10.74（提升18.7%），端到端时间从392.54秒降至357.02秒（1.10倍加速）。在LR=3e-3激进学习率下，AdamW退化至1885.24困惑度，而LBW-Guard保持在11.57。

Conclusion: 稳定性敏感的大语言模型训练可以从优化器之上的治理平面中获益，有界运行时控制能够在压力下保持高效计算，且该方法区别于优化器替换和局部梯度抑制，梯度裁剪基线无法复现其效果。

Abstract: Modern language-model training is increasingly exposed to instability, degraded runs, and wasted compute, especially under aggressive learning-rate, scale, and runtime-stress conditions. This paper introduces Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard), a bounded autonomous training-control governance layer that operates above AdamW. Rather than replacing the optimizer update rule, LBW-Guard observes training telemetry, interprets instability-sensitive regimes, and applies bounded control to optimizer execution while preserving fixed training objectives.
  We evaluate LBW-Guard in a Qwen2.5-centered stress-and-robustness suite using WikiText-103, with Qwen2.5-7B as the empirical anchor, model-size comparisons against Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-14B, learning-rate stress tests, gradient-clipping baselines, and a no-LoRA TinyLlama-1B full-parameter sanity check. In the 7B reference setting, LBW-Guard reduces final perplexity from 13.21 to 10.74, an 18.7% improvement, while reducing end-to-end time from 392.54s to 357.02s, a 1.10x speedup. Under stronger learning-rate stress, AdamW degrades to 1885.24 final perplexity at LR=3e-3 and 659.76 at LR=1e-3, whereas LBW-Guard remains trainable at 11.57 and 10.33, respectively. Gradient-clipping baselines do not reproduce this effect.
  These results support a scoped systems conclusion that stability-sensitive LLM training can benefit from a governance plane above the optimizer. LBW-Guard provides evidence that bounded runtime control can preserve productive compute under stress while remaining distinct from optimizer replacement and local gradient suppression.

</details>


### [172] [AgentNLQ: A General-Purpose Agent for Natural Language to SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19010)
*Olena Bogdanov,Yeunji Jung,Chandra Dhir,Pareekshitreddy Gaddam,Saurabh Jain,Lakshmi Tumati,Vijay Parthasarathy,Anup Shirgaonkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体NL2SQL方法，通过语义增强的模式表示、业务规则注入和多智能体协作（规划、编排、反思、自纠正），在BIRD基准上达到78.1%的语义准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在NL2SQL任务上的准确率尚未达到人类专家水平，需要进一步提升NL2SQL算法的性能。

Method: 提出多智能体方法，包含：(a) 基于LLM的优化编排器，实现规划、编排、反思和自纠正；(b) 先进模式增强方法，创建上下文感知元数据；(c) 注入用户提供的业务规则。

Result: 在BIRD基准上达到78.1%的语义准确率，并在不同领域和数据集上验证了方法的准确性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 该多智能体方法通过语义增强、业务规则引入和自纠正机制显著提升了NL2SQL的准确性，并具有良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Natural language to SQL (NL2SQL) conversion is an important problem for researchers and enterprises due to the ubiquitous importance of relational databases in broad-ranging practical problems. Despite the rapid advancements in the capabilities of LLMs, NL2SQL has not reached parity in accuracy with human expert SQL writers, hence needing additional improvements in NL2SQL algorithms. This study presents a new multi-agent method for NL2SQL that achieves 78.1% semantic accuracy on the BIg Bench for LaRge-scale Database (BIRD) benchmark. Our method leverages a semantically enriched representation of user-provided schema, adds user-provided business rules, and produces accurate SQL queries. The main contributions of this study are (a) We designed an optimized new orchestrator in a multi-agent solution that uses LLMs to plan, orchestrate, reflect, and self-correct to generate accurate SQL queries, (b) We developed an advanced schema enrichment method that creates context-aware metadata to improve accuracy, and (c) We demonstrated the accuracy and generalizability of the method across different domains and datasets by evaluating it on the BIRD-SQL benchmark.

</details>


### [173] [DecisionBench: A Benchmark for Emergent Delegation in Long-Horizon Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19099)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu,Zijian Carl Ma,Ao Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DecisionBench基准平台，用于评估长期智能体工作流中的委托(Delegation)能力。该平台固定任务集、模型池、委托接口、技能标注层和多维度指标集，通过5种参考条件下的全池扫描（23,375个任务实例）发现：1）质量指标无法捕捉编排信号；2）路由保真度在条件间差异大（7.5%-29.5%），传递通道主导描述内容；3）完美委托的反事实上限显示15-31个百分点的提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估单智能体能力，缺乏对长期工作流中智能体间委托（delegation）行为的系统评估框架，特别是质量单一指标无法反映编排质量，需要统一平台来比较不同委托策略。

Method: 构建DecisionBench：固定任务套件（GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL多轮）、11个模型7个供应商的同行池、call_model+read_profile委托接口、确定性技能标注层、多轴指标集。进行5种意识条件（无意识、自述、预估、真实）的全池扫描，共23,375个任务实例。

Result: 1）四种意识条件下端任务质量无统计显著性差异（|β|≤0.010, p≥0.21）；2）路由保真度@1范围7.5%-29.5%，传递通道（按需工具vs预加载描述）主导内容；3）完美委托反事实上限高于实测15-31个百分点。

Conclusion: 仅靠质量指标会遗漏编排信号；当前委托方法远未达到理论上限，存在巨大提升空间；传递通道比描述内容更关键。

Abstract: We introduce DecisionBench, a benchmark substrate for emergent delegation in long-horizon agentic workflows. The substrate fixes a task suite (GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL multi-turn), a peer-model pool (11 models, 7 vendor families), a delegation interface (call_model plus an optional read_profile channel), a deterministic skill-annotation layer, and a multi-axis metric suite covering quality, cost, latency, delegation rate, routing fidelity-at-k, vendor self-preference, and a counterfactual-delegation ceiling. The substrate is agnostic to how peer information is generated or delivered, so learned routers, richer peer memories, adaptive profile construction, and multi-step delegation can all be evaluated against it. We characterize the substrate with a five-condition reference sweep on the full pool (n=23,375 task instances). Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) mean end-task quality is statistically indistinguishable across the four awareness conditions (|beta| <= 0.010, p >= 0.21), so quality-only evaluation would miss the orchestration signal; (ii) routing fidelity-at-1 ranges from 7.5% to 29.5% across conditions at near-equal mean quality, with delivery channel (on-demand tool vs. preloaded description) dominating description content; (iii) a counterfactual ceiling places perfect delegation 15-31 percentage points above measured performance on every suite, locating large unrealized headroom for future orchestration methods. We release the substrate, annotation layer, reference intervention suite, analysis pipeline, and 220 per-condition run archives.

</details>


### [174] [How Far Are We From True Auto-Research?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19156)
*Zhengxin Zhang,Ning Wang,Sainyam Galhotra,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ResearchArena框架，用于系统评估自主论文生成智能体的质量。通过117篇智能体生成论文的多维度评审（纯稿件评审、工件感知同行评审、人工元评审），发现纯稿件评审结果乐观，但人工检查揭示论文在实验严谨性上存在严重问题（伪造结果、实验不足、计划与执行不匹配），所有论文均未达到顶会接收标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动研究系统虽能生成完整论文，但缺乏对智能体生成论文质量的系统性研究，需要建立标准化的评估框架来揭示其真实水平。

Method: 提出ResearchArena轻量级支架，让三种智能体（Claude Code/Opus 4.6、Codex/GPT-5.4、Kimi Code/K2.5）自主完成完整研究流程（构思、实验、写作、自我优化），在13个计算机科学种子问题上生成117篇论文，并通过三种互补评审视角（SAR纯稿件评审、工件感知同行评审PR、人工元评审）进行系统评估。

Result: SAR评审中Claude Code得分最高，优于FARS且匹配ICLR 2025人类投稿加权平均；但人工检查发现SAR与真实接收决策对齐差。PR评审下分数大幅下降，实验严谨性是主要瓶颈，表现为三种失败模式（伪造结果、实验不足、计划/执行不匹配），且智能体间差异显著（Codex伪造引用5-8% vs Kimi Code 72-77%）。117篇论文均未达到顶会接收标准。

Conclusion: 当前自主智能体生成的论文与真正自动研究仍有差距，纯稿件评审会高估其质量，实验严谨性是关键瓶颈，不同智能体展现出截然不同的研究人格。

Abstract: Recent auto-research systems can produce complete papers, but feasibility is not the same as quality, and the field still lacks a systematic study of how good agent-generated papers actually are. We introduce ResearchArena, a minimal scaffold that lets off-the-shelf agents (Claude Code using Opus 4.6, Codex using GPT-5.4, and Kimi Code using K2.5) carry out the full research loop themselves (ideation, experimentation, paper writing, self-refinement) under only lightweight guidance. Across 13 computer science seeds and 3 trials per agent-domain pair, ResearchArena yields 117 agent-generated papers, each evaluated under three complementary lenses: a manuscript-only reviewer (SAR), an artifact-aware peer review (PR) in which agents inspect the workspace alongside the manuscript, and an human conducted meta-review. Under SAR alone the picture is optimistic: Claude Code obtains the highest score, outperforms Analemma's FARS, and matches the weighted-average human ICLR 2025 submission, suggesting that minimally scaffolded agents can produce papers that look competitive on manuscript-only review. Manual inspection, however, reveals this picture is overstated: SAR scores are poorly aligned with its actual acceptance decisions and reward plausible framing without verifying experimental substance. Under artifact-aware PR scores drop sharply, and manual auditing identifies experimental rigor as the major bottleneck, decomposing into three failure modes (fabricated results, underpowered experiments, and plan/execution mismatch) that are highly agent-dependent: Codex 5%/8% paper-vs-artifact mismatch / fabricated references versus Kimi Code 77%/72%, a $\sim$15$\times$ spread that tracks distinct research personas the agents develop. None of the 117 agent-generated papers reaches the acceptance bar of a top-tier venue. This suggests that we are still gapped from the true auto-research.

</details>


### [175] [Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
*Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示环境不确定性的不同来源（波动性和随机性）对最优探索有相反影响：波动性促进探索，随机性抑制探索。作者通过扩展Gittins指数框架和高斯状态空间bandits建立了这一不对称性，并提出了CAUSE探索奖励方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将不同来源的环境不确定性视为等价，未区分它们对探索决策的不同影响。本文旨在阐明波动性（volatility）和随机性（stochasticity）如何以相反方向驱动最优探索。

Method: 扩展Gittins index框架到高斯状态空间bandits（含潜在动力学）；推导CAUSE（Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration）的闭式探索奖励，通过control-as-inference获得；与标准探索策略及Gittins-per-arm策略进行对比。

Result: 波动性增强探索，随机性抑制探索，两者呈不对称性；CAUSE在异构噪声环境中优于标准探索策略，也优于Gittins-per-arm策略（其rested-bandit最优性不适用于restless环境）。

Conclusion: 学习和探索受相同噪声推理不对称性支配；病理性噪声推理会产生反向而非单纯的受损探索，对精神疾病的计算模型具有启示意义。

Abstract: Adaptive decision-making in biological and artificial intelligence requires balancing the exploitation of known outcomes with the exploration of uncertain alternatives. Although prior work suggests that uncertainty generally promotes exploration, it has typically treated distinct sources of environmental uncertainty as equivalent. We consider environments with latent reward states that drift over time (volatility) and are observed through noisy outcomes (stochasticity). Both increase posterior uncertainty, yet we show they drive optimal exploration in opposite directions: volatility enhances it, stochasticity suppresses it. We establish this asymmetry formally by extending the Gittins index framework to Gaussian state-space bandits with latent dynamics. We further derive Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE), a closed-form exploration bonus obtained via control-as-inference that inherits the same monotonicities. CAUSE outperforms standard exploration strategies in environments with heterogeneous noise structure, and also improves on a Gittins-per-arm policy whose rested-bandit optimality does not transfer to restless settings. Learning and exploration are governed by the same noise-inference asymmetry, and the framework predicts that pathological noise inference produces \emph{reversed} rather than merely impaired exploration, with implications for computational accounts of psychiatric conditions.

</details>


### [176] [Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Guo,Christopher McCarty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个五阶段LLM集成框架，涵盖问卷设计、样本选择、预测试、缺失数据插补和收集后分析。在2024年佛罗里达州米尔顿飓风准备调查（n=946）中，提出的锚定边际理论知情LLM（A-TLM）在灾难相关的逐块非随机缺失条件下，RMSE优于三种经典插补基线，并实现了近乎零的符号偏差。研究还发现接近零的聚合偏差可能掩盖对立的子组误差，并提出了子组分层的偏差审计作为报告标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查研究面临响应率下降、样本偏差、高风险受访者的逐块缺失以及AI辅助欺诈完成等结构性挑战。LLM被提议作为解决方案，但在灾难背景下缺乏对完整调查工作流的严格评估。

Method: 提出五阶段LLM集成框架，包括：1）问卷设计、2）样本选择、3）预测试、4）缺失数据插补、5）收集后分析。引入保护动机理论（PMT）约束的共现知识图谱，开发了7种LLM配置（零样本推理、检索增强基线、理论知情变体），在n=946的佛罗里达居民调查数据上进行评估。

Result: A-TLM在灾难相关的逐块非随机缺失条件下RMSE优于IPW/MI、MICE+PMM、missForest三种基线（S4 RMSE 1.439 vs 1.496）；符号偏差接近零（-0.121），而随机森林插补的绝对偏差最大（-0.631）。围绕PMT因果结构组织检索并在单模型调用中整合所有证据优于非结构化检索和分阶段顺序推理（MAE 0.993 vs 1.097）。

Conclusion: 理论知情的结构化检索和多证据整合能显著提升LLM在调查数据处理中的表现；接近零的聚合偏差可能掩盖子组偏差，需进行子组分层的偏差审计；幻觉可通过基于检索约束的知识图谱和有根据的拒绝在架构上得到控制。

Abstract: Survey research faces mounting structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness among at-risk respondents, and AI-assisted fraudulent completions in online panels. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy, yet rigorous evaluations across the full survey workflow remain scarce, particularly in disaster contexts where data quality matters most. We present and evaluate a five-stage framework for LLM integration covering questionnaire design, sample selection, pilot testing, missing-data imputation, and post-collection analysis, using the 2024 Hurricane Milton preparedness survey of Florida residents (n=946) as a shared empirical testbed. We introduce a Protection Motivation Theory (PMT)-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph and develop seven LLM configurations spanning zero-shot inference, retrieval-augmented baselines, and novel theory-informed variants. Our proposed Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM) outperforms all three classical imputation baselines (IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest) on RMSE under disaster-relevant block-wise MNAR conditions (S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496 for the next-best), while achieving near-zero signed bias (-0.121) where the random-forest imputer produces the largest absolute bias (-0.631). Organizing retrieval around PMT causal structure and integrating all evidence in a single model call outperforms unstructured retrieval and staged sequential inference (MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097 for standard RAG). We document that near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors and propose subgroup-stratified bias auditing as a reporting standard. A retrieval-constrained knowledge-graph chatbot demonstrates that hallucination is architecturally manageable through grounded refusal.

</details>


### [177] [Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19250)
*Jinrui Jiang,Zhangtai Wu,Zhen Wu,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果分析发现多模态大模型中的模态冲突幻觉源于注意力头的不对称因果结构：驱动幻觉的注意力头分布广泛且权重较大，而抵抗幻觉的注意力头则集中且数量少。基于此提出MACI方法，通过条件性干预抑制驱动幻觉的注意力头，在多个模型上取得最佳幻觉抑制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多模态大语言模型在处理模态冲突时为何视觉证据无法战胜错误文本前提的内部机制，从因果视角探究驱动和抵抗幻觉的组件。

Method: 采用路径修补（path patching）方法对5个开源MLLMs进行头部级别的因果分析，识别出驱动幻觉和抵抗幻觉两类注意力头；提出MACI（Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention）方法，在检测到模态冲突时条件性地抑制被因果识别出的驱动幻觉注意力头。

Result: 发现驱动效应分布更广、聚合权重更大，而抵抗效应集中在少数高重要性头部。MACI在MMMC基准上实现最大幅度的幻觉减少，幻觉-准确率权衡优于其他推理时基线方法，并零样本迁移至SCI-SemanticConflict测试集。

Conclusion: 多模态幻觉源于分布式驱动与局部化抵抗的不平衡路由结构，通过条件性干预可有效缓解幻觉。

Abstract: Modality-conflict hallucination occurs when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) prioritize erroneous textual premises over contradictory visual evidence. To understand why visual evidence fails to prevail during generation, we take a mechanistic perspective and examine which internal components drive or resist this failure. We perform head-level causal analysis using path patching across five open-source MLLMs and identify two groups of attention heads with opposing causal roles: hallucination-driving heads and hallucination-resisting heads. We find a consistent asymmetry: driving effects are more broadly distributed and carry greater aggregate weight, whereas resisting effects concentrate in a small number of high-importance heads. Ablation experiments further confirm that these groups exert opposing effects during generation: distributed driving influence and localized resistance together form an imbalanced routing structure that biases generation toward the erroneous premise. Motivated by this finding, we propose MACI (Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention), a conditional intervention that suppresses causally identified hallucination-driving heads only when conflict is detected. Across five MLLMs, MACI achieves the largest hallucination reduction among compared inference-time baselines on the MMMC benchmark with a favorable hallucination-accuracy trade-off, and transfers zero-shot to the SCI-SemanticConflict test.

</details>


### [178] [AQuaUI: Visual Token Reduction for GUI Agents with Adaptive Quadtrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19260)
*Yuankai Li,Tinghui Zhu,Ha Min Son,Zhe Zhao,Xin Liu,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AQuaUI，一种无需训练的推理时令牌缩减方法，通过自适应四叉树利用GUI截图非均匀信息密度，在保持空间位置一致性的同时减少视觉令牌数量，实验表明可在保留99.06%性能下实现13.22%加速和29.52%令牌减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMM驱动的GUI智能体模型在每次迭代中引入高分辨率截图，但GUI截图存在高度非均匀的空间信息密度（大面积冗余区域与关键高保真区域并存）。现有方法要么需要额外训练，要么依赖基于注意力的令牌压缩，忽略了GUI截图的结构化布局和空间冗余性。

Method: 1) 自适应四叉树构建：对每张截图输入构建自适应四叉树，每个叶子节点保留一个代表性合并令牌；2) 空间位置保持：保留令牌的空间位置贯穿整个流水线，确保所有位置编码阶段的一致性；3) 条件四叉树算法：利用多步GUI交互中连续截图的连续性，以先前四叉树为参考优化当前四叉树，保持静态或轻微变化GUI区域中的细粒度区域。

Result: 在标准基础定位和导航基准上，相比先前基线方法实现了更优的准确率-效率权衡。在GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct上，实现13.22%加速、29.52%视觉令牌减少，同时保留99.06%的全令牌性能。

Conclusion: GUI截图的空间冗余性可以在不重新训练的情况下在推理阶段被利用，AQuaUI提供了一种有效的训练无关推理时令牌缩减方法。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently emerged as promising backbones for GUI-agent models, where high-resolution GUI screenshots are introduced to the prompts at each iteration step. However, these screenshots exhibit highly non-uniform spatial information density: large regions may carry little information and are visually homogeneous, while key text and icons may require high visual fidelity. Existing approaches to this problem either require additional training or rely on attention-based token compression, ignoring the structured layout and spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots. To fill the gap, this paper proposes AquaUI, a training-free inference-time token reduction method for GUI agent models that utilizes the non-uniform information density in screenshots. AQuaUI constructs an adaptive quadtree on each screenshot input and keeps one representative merged token per leaf of the quadtree. AQuaUI preserves the spatial positions of retained tokens throughout the pipeline to ensure that all position-encoding stages remain consistent. To further improve temporal consistency across multi-step GUI interactions, we propose a conditional quadtree algorithm that leverages the continuity between consecutive screenshots within a single request. Specifically, it refines the current quadtree using previous quadtrees as references, helping preserve fine-grained regions across static or mildly shifted GUI states. We implement AQuaUI on state-of-the-art GUI agent models and conduct experiments on standard grounding and navigational benchmarks. AQuaUI consistently shows improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over prior baselines. Notably, on GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct, AQuaUI achieves up to 13.22% speedup and 29.52% fewer visual tokens while retaining 99.06% of full-token performance, suggesting that the spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots can be exploited at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [179] [MOCHA: Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19330)
*Md Mehrab Tanjim,Jayakumar Subramanian,Xiang Chen,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Anlan Zhang,Sungchul Kim,Somdeb Sarkhel,Sunav Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MOCHA，一种基于切比雪夫标量化和指数退火的多目标提示优化方法，用于LLM agent技能优化，在满足平台约束的同时提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化器忽略或简单加权处理多目标约束（任务性能与平台限制之间的权衡），无法在非凸目标区域找到帕累托最优的技能变体。

Method: 用切比雪夫标量化替代单目标选择，覆盖完整帕累托前沿（包括非凸区域）；结合指数退火策略，从探索过渡到利用。

Result: 在6个agent技能任务中，现有优化器在4个任务上无改善，MOCHA在所有任务上均取得提升，平均正确率相对提升7.5%（FEVER达14.9%，TheoremQA达10.4%），发现2倍以上帕累托最优技能变体。

Conclusion: MOCHA有效解决了多目标技能优化问题，在遵循平台约束的同时显著提升任务性能。

Abstract: LLM agents organize behavior through skills - structured natural-language specifications governing how an agent reasons, retrieves, and responds. Unlike monolithic prompts, skills are multi-field artifacts subject to hard platform constraints: description fields are truncated for routing, instruction bodies are compacted via progressive disclosure, and co-resident skills compete for limited context windows.
